"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend password-reset-request mutation wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSubmitting(false);
      setSubmitted(true);
    }, 500);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-md px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
        <EmptyState
          title="Check your email"
          description={`If an account exists for ${email}, we've sent a link to reset your password.`}
          action={
            <Link href="/login">
              <Button variant="secondary">Back to login</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Forgot password</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Enter your email and we&apos;ll send you a link to reset your password.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="you@example.com"
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={submitting}>
          Send reset link
        </Button>
      </form>

      <p className="mt-8 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Remembered your password?{" "}
        <Link href="/login" className="font-semibold text-gold-dark hover:underline">
          Log in
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\login\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, SocialLoginButtons } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useLogin } from "@saachi/api-client";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const login = useLogin();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    login.mutate(
      { email, password },
      {
        onSuccess: (session) =>
          router.push(session.role === "instructor" ? "/instructor/dashboard" : "/dashboard")
      }
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Log in</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Welcome back to Saachi AI.</p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="you@example.com"
        />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={login.isPending}>
          Log in
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <SocialLoginButtons />
      </div>

      <p className="mt-8 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Don&apos;t have an account?{" "}
        <Link href="/register" className="font-semibold text-gold-dark hover:underline">
          Sign up
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\register\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, Select, SocialLoginButtons } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useRegister } from "@saachi/api-client";

const ROLE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Learner - I want to learn AI skills", value: "learner" },
  { label: "Instructor - I want to teach and sell courses", value: "instructor" }
];

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const register = useRegister();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("learner");

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (password !== confirmPassword) return;
    register.mutate(
      { fullName, email, password, confirmPassword, role: role as "learner" | "instructor" },
      { onSuccess: () => router.push(role === "instructor" ? "/instructor/dashboard" : "/dashboard") }
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Create your account</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Join the Saachi AI marketplace.</p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Full name"
          required
          value={fullName}
          onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Your full name"
        />
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="you@example.com"
        />
        <Select label="I am joining as" options={ROLE_OPTIONS} value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)} />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
        />
        <Input
          label="Confirm password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={confirmPassword}
          onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
          error={confirmPassword && password !== confirmPassword ? "Passwords do not match" : undefined}
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={register.isPending}>
          Create account
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <SocialLoginButtons />
      </div>

      <p className="mt-8 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Already have an account?{" "}
        <Link href="/login" className="font-semibold text-gold-dark hover:underline">
          Log in
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\reset-password\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent, Suspense } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function ResetPasswordForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const token = searchParams.get("token");

  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (password !== confirmPassword) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend password-reset-confirm mutation wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSubmitting(false);
      setDone(true);
    }, 500);
  }

  if (!token) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="Invalid or expired link"
        description="This password reset link is missing or no longer valid."
        action={
          <Link href="/forgot-password">
            <Button>Request a new link</Button>
          </Link>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  if (done) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="Password updated"
        description="Your password has been reset. You can now log in."
        action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/login")}>Go to login</Button>}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Reset password</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Choose a new password for your account.</p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="New password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
        />
        <Input
          label="Confirm new password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={confirmPassword}
          onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
          error={confirmPassword && password !== confirmPassword ? "Passwords do not match" : undefined}
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={submitting}>
          Reset password
        </Button>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <ResetPasswordForm />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\verify-email\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Suspense, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { MailCheck, MailWarning } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Loading, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function VerifyEmailContent() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const token = searchParams.get("token");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"checking" | "success" | "error">("checking");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!token) {
      setStatus("error");
      return;
    }
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend email-verification mutation wired up yet.
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setStatus("success"), 800);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [token]);

  if (status === "checking") {
    return <Loading label="Verifying your email..." />;
  }

  if (status === "error") {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="Verification link invalid"
        description="This email verification link is missing or has expired."
        action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/login")}>Back to login</Button>}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
      <div className="mb-4 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-light text-teal">
        <MailCheck size={26} />
      </div>
      <h1 className="mb-2 font-display text-[24px] font-bold text-ink">Email verified</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 max-w-sm text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Your email address has been confirmed. You can now access your account.
      </p>
      <Button onClick={() => router.push("/dashboard")}>Go to dashboard</Button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function VerifyEmailPage() {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-20 md:px-0">
      <Suspense
        fallback={
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center text-ink-soft">
            <MailWarning size={26} className="mb-3" />
            Loading...
          </div>
        }
      >
        <VerifyEmailContent />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\courses\new\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, Select, Textarea, EmptyState, cn } from "@saachi/ui";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../../../../lib/categories";

const STEPS = ["Basics", "Curriculum", "Pricing", "Media", "Review & Submit"];

const LEVEL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Beginner", value: "beginner" },
  { label: "Intermediate", value: "intermediate" },
  { label: "Advanced", value: "advanced" }
];

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "English", value: "en" },
  { label: "Urdu", value: "ur" }
];

type LessonType = "video" | "text" | "quiz" | "resource";

type Lesson = { id: string; title: string; type: LessonType };
type Module = { id: string; title: string; lessons: Lesson[] };

let idCounter = 0;
function nextId() {
  idCounter += 1;
  return `m${idCounter}`;
}

export default function CourseWizardPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  // Step 1: Basics
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [subtitle, setSubtitle] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(CATEGORIES[0]?.id ?? "");
  const [level, setLevel] = useState("beginner");
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState("en");

  // Step 2: Curriculum
  const [modules, setModules] = useState<Module[]>([]);

  function addModule() {
    setModules((prev) => [...prev, { id: nextId(), title: `Module ${prev.length + 1}`, lessons: [] }]);
  }

  function addLesson(moduleId: string, type: LessonType) {
    setModules((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) =>
        m.id === moduleId
          ? { ...m, lessons: [...m.lessons, { id: nextId(), title: `New ${type} lesson`, type }] }
          : m
      )
    );
  }

  // Step 3: Pricing
  const [price, setPrice] = useState("");
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState("USD");
  const [includeInSubscription, setIncludeInSubscription] = useState(false);

  // Step 4: Media
  const [thumbnailUrl, setThumbnailUrl] = useState("");
  const [promoVideoUrl, setPromoVideoUrl] = useState("");

  const canGoNext = (() => {
    if (step === 0) return title.trim().length > 0 && category.length > 0;
    if (step === 1) return modules.length > 0;
    if (step === 2) return price.trim().length > 0;
    return true;
  })();

  function handleSubmit() {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend course-creation mutation wired up yet.
    setSubmitted(true);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg py-16">
        <EmptyState
          title="Course submitted for review"
          description="Our moderation team will review your course and notify you once it's approved."
          action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/instructor/courses")}>Back to my courses</Button>}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Create a new course</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Step {step + 1} of {STEPS.length}: {STEPS[step]}</p>

      {/* Stepper */}
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-2">
        {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
          <div key={s} className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2">
            <div
              className={cn(
                "flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold",
                i < step ? "bg-teal text-white" : i === step ? "bg-gold-dark text-white" : "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft"
              )}
            >
              {i < step ? <Check size={14} /> : i + 1}
            </div>
            {i < STEPS.length - 1 && <div className={cn("h-0.5 flex-1", i < step ? "bg-teal" : "bg-line")} />}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        {step === 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <Input label="Course title" required value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. n8n Automation Bootcamp" />
            <Input label="Subtitle" value={subtitle} onChange={(e) => setSubtitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="A short one-line summary" />
            <Select
              label="Category"
              options={CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id }))}
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Select label="Level" options={LEVEL_OPTIONS} value={level} onChange={(e) => setLevel(e.target.value)} />
              <Select label="Language" options={LANGUAGE_OPTIONS} value={language} onChange={(e) => setLanguage(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 1 && (
          <div>
            {modules.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState title="No modules yet" description="Add your first module to start building the curriculum." />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                {modules.map((m) => (
                  <div key={m.id} className="rounded-control border border-line p-4">
                    <p className="mb-3 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.title}</p>
                    {m.lessons.length > 0 && (
                      <ul className="mb-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                        {m.lessons.map((l) => (
                          <li key={l.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                            <span className="rounded bg-paper-dim px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] uppercase">{l.type}</span>
                            {l.title}
                          </li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    )}
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "video")}>+ Video</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "text")}>+ Text</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "quiz")}>+ Quiz</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "resource")}>+ Resource</Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            <Button variant="secondary" className="mt-4" onClick={addModule}>+ Add module</Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 2 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Input label="Price" type="number" required value={price} onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)} placeholder="49.00" />
              <Select
                label="Currency"
                options={[
                  { label: "USD", value: "USD" },
                  { label: "PKR", value: "PKR" }
                ]}
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={includeInSubscription}
                onChange={(e) => setIncludeInSubscription(e.target.checked)}
                className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
              />
              Include this course in the Saachi AI Pro subscription
            </label>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 3 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <Input label="Thumbnail URL" value={thumbnailUrl} onChange={(e) => setThumbnailUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://..." />
            <Input label="Promo video URL" value={promoVideoUrl} onChange={(e) => setPromoVideoUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://..." />
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
              File upload with resumable/chunked upload will be wired up once the signed-URL flow from the backend is available.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 4 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 text-[14px]">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Title</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{title || "â€”"}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Category</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.id === category)?.name ?? "â€”"}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Modules</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{modules.length} module(s), {modules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.lessons.length, 0)} lesson(s)</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Price</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{price ? `${price} ${currency}` : "â€”"}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-between">
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={step === 0} onClick={() => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1))}>
          Back
        </Button>
        {step < STEPS.length - 1 ? (
          <Button disabled={!canGoNext} onClick={() => setStep((s) => Math.min(STEPS.length - 1, s + 1))}>
            Next
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <Button onClick={handleSubmit}>Submit for review</Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\courses\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button, EmptyState, Select, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

type CourseStatus = "draft" | "pending_review" | "published" | "rejected" | "archived";

type InstructorCourse = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  status: CourseStatus;
  enrollments: number;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorCourses() once
// packages/api-client's instructor-course hooks are confirmed.
const COURSES: InstructorCourse[] = [
  { id: "crs_i1", title: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", status: "published", enrollments: 654, price: 39, currency: "USD" },
  { id: "crs_i2", title: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support", status: "pending_review", enrollments: 0, price: 59, currency: "USD" },
  { id: "crs_i3", title: "Prompt Engineering for Client Work", status: "draft", enrollments: 0, price: 29, currency: "USD" },
  { id: "crs_i4", title: "Cold Outreach with Claude", status: "rejected", enrollments: 0, price: 35, currency: "USD" }
];

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "All", value: "all" },
  { label: "Draft", value: "draft" },
  { label: "Pending review", value: "pending_review" },
  { label: "Published", value: "published" },
  { label: "Rejected", value: "rejected" },
  { label: "Archived", value: "archived" }
];

// NOTE: only using Badge variants confirmed to exist ("gold" | "success" | "error").
// Draft/archived fall back to no variant prop (component default styling).
const STATUS_VARIANT: Partial<Record<CourseStatus, "gold" | "success" | "error">> = {
  pending_review: "gold",
  published: "success",
  rejected: "error"
};

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<CourseStatus, string> = {
  draft: "Draft",
  pending_review: "Pending review",
  published: "Published",
  rejected: "Rejected",
  archived: "Archived"
};

export default function InstructorMyCoursesPage() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");

  const filtered = COURSES.filter((c) => status === "all" || c.status === status);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">My Courses</h1>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Select options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} className="w-48" />
          <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
            <Button>New course</Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No courses yet"
          description="Create your first course to start selling on Saachi AI."
          action={
            <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
              <Button>Create a course</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
            <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Course</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Status</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Enrollments</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Price</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((c) => (
                <tr key={c.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-medium text-ink">{c.title}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    {STATUS_VARIANT[c.status] ? (
                      <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANT[c.status]}>{STATUS_LABEL[c.status]}</Badge>
                    ) : (
                      <Badge>{STATUS_LABEL[c.status]}</Badge>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{c.enrollments}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-mono text-ink">
                    {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: c.currency }).format(c.price)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <Link href={`/instructor/courses/${c.id}/edit`} className="text-gold-dark hover:underline">
                      Edit
                    </Link>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\mentorship\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CalendarDays, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, Select, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type Slot = { id: string; day: string; time: string };

const DAY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Monday", value: "Mon" },
  { label: "Tuesday", value: "Tue" },
  { label: "Wednesday", value: "Wed" },
  { label: "Thursday", value: "Thu" },
  { label: "Friday", value: "Fri" },
  { label: "Saturday", value: "Sat" },
  { label: "Sunday", value: "Sun" }
];

let slotCounter = 0;

export default function InstructorMentorshipSettingsPage() {
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [hourlyRate, setHourlyRate] = useState("40");
  const [sessionLength, setSessionLength] = useState("30");
  const [day, setDay] = useState("Mon");
  const [time, setTime] = useState("");
  const [slots, setSlots] = useState<Slot[]>([]);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  function addSlot() {
    if (!time.trim()) return;
    slotCounter += 1;
    setSlots((prev) => [...prev, { id: `slot${slotCounter}`, day, time }]);
    setTime("");
  }

  function removeSlot(id: string) {
    setSlots((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== id));
  }

  function handleSave() {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend mentorship-settings mutation wired up yet.
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 3000);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Mentorship Settings</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Offer paid 1-on-1 mentorship sessions to learners.</p>

      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Enable mentorship</p>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Allow learners to book paid sessions with you.</p>
        </div>
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={enabled}
          onChange={(e) => setEnabled(e.target.checked)}
          aria-label="Toggle mentorship availability"
          className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
        />
      </div>

      {enabled && (
        <>
          <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Input label="Hourly rate (USD)" type="number" value={hourlyRate} onChange={(e) => setHourlyRate(e.target.value)} />
            <Select
              label="Session length (minutes)"
              options={[
                { label: "30 minutes", value: "30" },
                { label: "45 minutes", value: "45" },
                { label: "60 minutes", value: "60" }
              ]}
              value={sessionLength}
              onChange={(e) => setSessionLength(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
            <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
              <CalendarDays size={16} />
              Weekly availability
            </h2>

            {slots.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState title="No availability set" description="Add time slots when learners can book you." />
            ) : (
              <ul className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
                {slots.map((s) => (
                  <li key={s.id} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-control bg-paper-dim px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink">
                    {s.day} â€” {s.time}
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => removeSlot(s.id)} aria-label="Remove slot" className="text-ink-soft hover:text-error">
                      <Trash2 size={14} />
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2">
              <Select options={DAY_OPTIONS} value={day} onChange={(e) => setDay(e.target.value)} className="w-36" />
              <Input value={time} onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. 10:00 AM" className="w-40" />
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={addSlot}>
                <Plus size={14} className="mr-1.5" />
                Add slot
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <Button size="lg" className="mt-6" onClick={handleSave}>
        {saved ? "Saved" : "Save settings"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\promotions\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Rocket, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Select, EmptyState, Badge, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

type PackageTier = "bronze" | "silver" | "gold";

const PACKAGES: {
  tier: PackageTier;
  name: string;
  price: string;
  perks: string[];
}[] = [
  { tier: "bronze", name: "Bronze Boost", price: "$19", perks: ["Category page pinning (7 days)", "Basic visibility badge"] },
  { tier: "silver", name: "Silver Boost", price: "$49", perks: ["Everything in Bronze", "Top-of-search placement (7 days)", "Silver visibility badge"] },
  { tier: "gold", name: "Gold Boost", price: "$99", perks: ["Everything in Silver", "Homepage featured slot (7 days)", "Gold visibility badge"] }
];

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorCourses() once confirmed.
const COURSE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Select a course...", value: "" },
  { label: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", value: "crs_i1" },
  { label: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support", value: "crs_i2" }
];

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorPromotions() once confirmed.
const ACTIVE_PROMOTIONS: { id: string; courseTitle: string; tier: PackageTier; expiresIn: string }[] = [
  { id: "promo_1", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", tier: "silver", expiresIn: "4 days" }
];

export default function InstructorPromotionsPage() {
  const [selectedCourse, setSelectedCourse] = useState("");
  const [purchasing, setPurchasing] = useState<PackageTier | null>(null);
  const [purchased, setPurchased] = useState<PackageTier | null>(null);

  function handlePurchase(tier: PackageTier) {
    if (!selectedCourse) return;
    setPurchasing(tier);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend promotion-purchase mutation wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setPurchasing(null);
      setPurchased(tier);
    }, 700);
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Promotions</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Boost your course visibility with a promotion package.</p>

      <div className="mb-8 max-w-sm">
        <Select
          label="Course to promote"
          options={COURSE_OPTIONS}
          value={selectedCourse}
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedCourse(e.target.value)}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-3">
        {PACKAGES.map((pkg) => (
          <div
            key={pkg.tier}
            className={cn(
              "flex flex-col rounded-card border p-6",
              pkg.tier === "gold" ? "border-gold-dark/50 bg-gold-tint" : "border-line bg-card"
            )}
          >
            {pkg.tier === "gold" && (
              <Badge variant="gold" className="mb-3 self-start">
                Best value
              </Badge>
            )}
            <h2 className="font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">{pkg.name}</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[24px] font-bold text-ink">{pkg.price}</p>
            <ul className="my-5 flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              {pkg.perks.map((perk) => (
                <li key={perk} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <Check size={14} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-teal" />
                  {perk}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <Button
              variant={pkg.tier === "gold" ? "primary" : "secondary"}
              disabled={!selectedCourse || purchasing !== null}
              onClick={() => handlePurchase(pkg.tier)}
              className="w-full"
            >
              <Rocket size={14} className="mr-1.5" />
              {purchased === pkg.tier ? "Purchased" : purchasing === pkg.tier ? "Processing..." : "Boost course"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <h2 className="mb-4 mt-12 font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Active promotions</h2>
      {ACTIVE_PROMOTIONS.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No active promotions" description="Purchased boost packages will appear here." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {ACTIVE_PROMOTIONS.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{p.courseTitle}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Expires in {p.expiresIn}</p>
              </div>
              <Badge variant={p.tier === "gold" ? "gold" : undefined}>{p.tier} boost</Badge>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\reviews\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Star } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Textarea, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type Review = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  author: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  reply?: string;
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorReviews() once confirmed.
const REVIEWS: Review[] = [
  {
    id: "rev_i1",
    courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI",
    author: "Bilal S.",
    rating: 5,
    comment: "Very practical, loved it. The invoice parsing workflow alone was worth the price."
  },
  {
    id: "rev_i2",
    courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI",
    author: "Fatima R.",
    rating: 4,
    comment: "Good pacing, clear examples. Wish there was a bit more on error handling.",
    reply: "Thanks Fatima! Adding an error-handling lesson is on my list for the next update."
  },
  {
    id: "rev_i3",
    courseTitle: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support",
    author: "Omar Sheikh",
    rating: 5,
    comment: "Best WhatsApp bot course I've taken - clear, practical, and well structured."
  }
];

export default function InstructorReviewsPage() {
  const [replies, setReplies] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [submittedIds, setSubmittedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

  function handleReplyChange(id: string, value: string) {
    setReplies((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: value }));
  }

  function handleSubmitReply(id: string) {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend review-reply mutation wired up yet.
    setSubmittedIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(id));
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Reviews</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">View and respond to reviews on your courses.</p>

      {REVIEWS.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No reviews yet" description="Reviews from learners on your courses will appear here." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {REVIEWS.map((r) => (
            <div key={r.id} className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
              <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{r.courseTitle}</p>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-gold-dark">
                  {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                    <Star key={i} size={13} fill={i < r.rating ? "currentColor" : "none"} />
                  ))}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mb-1 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft">{r.author}</p>
              <p className="mb-3 text-[14px] text-ink">{r.comment}</p>

              {r.reply || submittedIds.has(r.id) ? (
                <div className="rounded-control bg-paper-dim px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Your reply: </span>
                  {r.reply ?? replies[r.id]}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <Textarea
                    rows={2}
                    placeholder="Write a reply..."
                    value={replies[r.id] ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => handleReplyChange(r.id, e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <Button size="sm" className="self-start" disabled={!replies[r.id]?.trim()} onClick={() => handleSubmitReply(r.id)}>
                    Post reply
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Input, Textarea, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function InstructorSettingsPage() {

  const [bio, setBio] = useState("");
  const [portfolioUrl, setPortfolioUrl] = useState("");
  const [taxId, setTaxId] = useState("");
  const [emailNotifs, setEmailNotifs] = useState(true);
  const [qaNotifs, setQaNotifs] = useState(true);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your instructor settings." />;
  }

  function handleSave() {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend instructor-settings mutation wired up yet.

    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 3000);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Instructor Settings</h1>

        <Input label="Tax ID / NTN (optional)" value={taxId} onChange={(e) => setTaxId(e.target.value)} />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Notification preferences</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Email me about new sales</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={emailNotifs}
              onChange={(e) => setEmailNotifs(e.target.checked)}
              aria-label="Toggle sales email notifications"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Email me about new Q&amp;A questions</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={qaNotifs}
              onChange={(e) => setQaNotifs(e.target.checked)}
              aria-label="Toggle Q&A email notifications"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Button size="lg" className="mt-6" onClick={handleSave}>
        {saved ? "Saved" : "Save changes"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { MessageCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

type Student = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  progressPercent: number;
  lastActive: string;
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorStudents() once confirmed.
const STUDENTS: Student[] = [
  { id: "stu_1", name: "Hina Malik", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", progressPercent: 100, lastActive: "2 days ago" },
  { id: "stu_2", name: "Omar Sheikh", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", progressPercent: 65, lastActive: "Today" },
  { id: "stu_3", name: "Sara Iqbal", courseTitle: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support", progressPercent: 40, lastActive: "5 days ago" },
  { id: "stu_4", name: "Noor Fatima", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", progressPercent: 100, lastActive: "1 week ago" },
  { id: "stu_5", name: "Ali Raza", courseTitle: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support", progressPercent: 20, lastActive: "Yesterday" }
];

export default function InstructorStudentsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  const filtered = STUDENTS.filter((s) =>
    s.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || s.courseTitle.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Students</h1>
        <Input
          placeholder="Search students or courses..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="w-64"
        />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No students yet"
          description="Once learners enroll in your courses, they'll appear here."
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
            <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Student</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Course</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Progress</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Last active</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((s) => (
                <tr key={s.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-medium text-ink">{s.name}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{s.courseTitle}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="h-1.5 w-24 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                        <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${s.progressPercent}%` }} />
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-ink-soft">{s.progressPercent}%</span>
                      {s.progressPercent === 100 && <Badge variant="success">Done</Badge>}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{s.lastActive}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                      <MessageCircle size={13} className="mr-1.5" />
                      Message
                    </Button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\verification\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ShieldCheck, UploadCloud, Clock, CheckCircle2, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, Badge, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

type VerificationStatus = "not_submitted" | "pending" | "verified" | "rejected";

export default function InstructorVerificationPage() {
  // NOTE: demo-only local state. Replace with useInstructorVerification() once confirmed.
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<VerificationStatus>("not_submitted");
  const [idFileName, setIdFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [portfolioUrl, setPortfolioUrl] = useState("");
  const [introVideoUrl, setIntroVideoUrl] = useState("");

  function handleIdSelect(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) setIdFileName(file.name);
  }

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend verification-submission mutation wired up yet.
    setStatus("pending");
  }

  const STATUS_META: Record<VerificationStatus, { label: string; variant?: "gold" | "success" | "error"; icon: typeof Clock }> = {
    not_submitted: { label: "Not submitted", icon: ShieldCheck },
    pending: { label: "Pending review", variant: "gold", icon: Clock },
    verified: { label: "Verified", variant: "success", icon: CheckCircle2 },
    rejected: { label: "Rejected", variant: "error", icon: XCircle }
  };

  const meta = STATUS_META[status];
  const Icon = meta.icon;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Verification Status</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Get verified to unlock a lower commission tier and a trust badge.</p>

      <div
        className={cn(
          "mb-8 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-card border p-5",
          status === "verified" ? "border-success/30 bg-success-tint" :
          status === "rejected" ? "border-error/30 bg-error-tint" :
          status === "pending" ? "border-gold-dark/30 bg-gold-tint" :
          "border-line bg-card"
        )}
      >
        <Icon size={22} className="shrink-0 text-ink" />
        <div className="flex-1">
          <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Current status</p>
          {meta.variant ? (
            <Badge variant={meta.variant} className="mt-1">{meta.label}</Badge>
          ) : (
            <Badge className="mt-1">{meta.label}</Badge>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {(status === "not_submitted" || status === "rejected") && (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="mb-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink">Government ID or business registration</p>
            <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line px-4 py-3 text-[13px] text-ink-soft hover:border-gold-dark/40">
              <UploadCloud size={16} />
              {idFileName ?? "Upload ID document"}
              <input type="file" accept="image/*,.pdf" className="hidden" onChange={handleIdSelect} required />
            </label>
          </div>

          <Input
            label="Portfolio / proof-of-work link"
            required
            value={portfolioUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setPortfolioUrl(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="GitHub, LinkedIn, or past project samples"
          />

          <Input
            label="Intro video link (optional)"
            value={introVideoUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setIntroVideoUrl(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="A short video introducing yourself"
          />

          <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="self-start" disabled={!idFileName || !portfolioUrl.trim()}>
            Submit for verification
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}

      {status === "pending" && (
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Your submission is under review. This typically takes 2-3 business days.
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\_components\InstructorShell.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Menu, X, LogOut, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Bell, ChevronDown, Settings, User } from "lucide-react";
import { ThemeToggle } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { INSTRUCTOR_NAV } from "../../../lib/instructor-nav-config";

function cx(...parts: Array<string | false | undefined>) {
  return parts.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

type NotificationItem = { id: string; label: string; href: string };

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorNotifications() once confirmed.
const NOTIFICATIONS: NotificationItem[] = [
  { id: "n1", label: 'New review on "Automate Everything with n8n & AI"', href: "/instructor/reviews" },
  { id: "n2", label: "2 new students enrolled this week", href: "/instructor/students" },
  { id: "n3", label: "Your Silver Boost promotion expires in 4 days", href: "/instructor/promotions" }
];

export function InstructorShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();

  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [notifOpen, setNotifOpen] = useState(false);
  const [userMenuOpen, setUserMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const notifRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const userMenuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (notifRef.current && !notifRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setNotifOpen(false);
      if (userMenuRef.current && !userMenuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setUserMenuOpen(false);
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  function handleLogout() {
    logout.mutate(undefined, { onSuccess: () => router.push("/login") });
  }

  const sidebarContent = (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col pt-[65px]">
      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-5 overflow-y-auto px-3 pb-4 pt-4">
        {INSTRUCTOR_NAV.map((section) => (
          <div key={section.title}>
            {!collapsed && (
              <div className="mb-1.5 px-2.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink-soft/60">
                {section.title}
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
              {section.items.map((item) => {
                const active = pathname === item.href || Boolean(pathname?.startsWith(`${item.href}/`));
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={item.href}
                    href={item.href}
                    onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                    title={collapsed ? item.label : undefined}
                    className={cx(
                      "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                      collapsed && "justify-center",
                      active ? "bg-gold text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                    )}
                  >
                    <item.icon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0" />
                    {!collapsed && <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>}
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </nav>

      <div className="border-t border-line px-3 py-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setCollapsed((v) => !v)}
          className={cx(
            "mb-1 hidden w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink md:flex",
            collapsed && "justify-center"
          )}
        >
          {collapsed ? <ChevronRight size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <ChevronLeft size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          {!collapsed && "Collapse"}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleLogout}
          className={cx(
            "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error",
            collapsed && "justify-center"
          )}
        >
          <LogOut size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          {!collapsed && "Log out"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-paper">
      <div
        className={cx(
          "hidden shrink-0 border-r border-line bg-card transition-[width] duration-200 ease-out md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:flex",
          collapsed ? "w-[76px]" : "w-[248px]"
        )}
      >
        {sidebarContent}
      </div>

      <div className="sticky top-0 z-40 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-line bg-card/95 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur-sm md:px-6">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
            aria-label="Open menu"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink md:hidden"
          >
            <Menu size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>

          <Link href="/instructor/dashboard" className="hidden shrink-0 items-center gap-2 sm:flex">
            <span className="font-display text-[16px] font-bold text-ink">
              Saachi<span className="text-gold-dark">AI</span>
            </span>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft">
              Instructor
            </span>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
          <div className="relative" ref={notifRef}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setNotifOpen((v) => !v)}
              aria-label="Notifications"
              className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
            >
              <Bell size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
              {NOTIFICATIONS.length > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute right-1.5 top-1.5 flex h-4 min-w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold-dark px-1 text-[9px] font-semibold text-white">
                  {NOTIFICATIONS.length}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>

            {notifOpen && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] w-[300px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg">
                <div className="border-b border-line px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">Notifications</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col">
                  {NOTIFICATIONS.map((n) => (
                    <Link
                      key={n.id}
                      href={n.href}
                      onClick={() => setNotifOpen(false)}
                      className="flex items-start gap-2.5 border-b border-line px-4 py-3 text-[12.5px] text-ink transition-colors duration-100 ease-out last:border-0 hover:bg-paper-dim"
                    >
                      <span className="mt-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gold-dark" />
                      <span>{n.label}</span>
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <ThemeToggle />

          <div className="relative" ref={userMenuRef}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen((v) => !v)}
              className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-control px-1.5 py-1 transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
            >
              <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-line bg-gold-tint text-[12px] font-semibold text-gold-dark">
                {session?.avatarUrl ? (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img src={session.avatarUrl} alt={session.fullName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                ) : (
                  (session?.fullName ?? "I").charAt(0)
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="hidden min-w-0 text-left sm:block">
                <div className="truncate text-[12.5px] font-semibold leading-tight text-ink">
                  {session?.fullName ?? "Instructor"}
                </div>
                <div className="truncate text-[11px] leading-tight text-ink-soft">Instructor</div>
              </div>
              <ChevronDown size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="hidden shrink-0 text-ink-soft sm:block" />
            </button>

            {userMenuOpen && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] w-[220px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg">
                <div className="border-b border-line px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{session?.fullName ?? "Instructor"}</p>
                  <p className="truncate text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{session?.email}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col p-1.5">
                  <Link
                    href="/instructor/settings"
                    onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                  >
                    <Settings size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Settings
                  </Link>
                  <Link
                    href="/dashboard"
                    onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                  >
                    <User size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Switch to learner view
                  </Link>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleLogout}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-left text-[13px] text-error transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-error-tint"
                  >
                    <LogOut size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Log out
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex md:hidden">
          <div
            className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40 transition-opacity duration-200 ease-out"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
          />
          <div className="relative flex h-full w-[280px] flex-col bg-card transition-transform duration-200 ease-out">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              aria-label="Close menu"
              className="absolute right-3 top-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
            >
              <X size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
            {sidebarContent}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className={cx("transition-[padding] duration-200 ease-out", collapsed ? "md:pl-[76px]" : "md:pl-[248px]")}>
        <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">{children}</main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\become-instructor\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { GraduationCap } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState, Input, Textarea } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function BecomeInstructorPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [bio, setBio] = useState("");
  const [expertise, setExpertise] = useState("");
  const [portfolioUrl, setPortfolioUrl] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to apply as an instructor." />;
  }

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend application-review workflow wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSubmitting(false);
      setSubmitted(true);
    }, 600);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg py-16">
        <EmptyState
          title="Application submitted"
          description="Our team will review your instructor application and get back to you by email."
          action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/dashboard")}>Back to dashboard</Button>}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
      <h1 className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">
        <GraduationCap size={24} />
        Become an instructor
      </h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Tell us about your expertise. Applications are reviewed manually before approval.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Textarea
          label="Bio"
          required
          rows={4}
          value={bio}
          onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Tell learners about your background and experience"
        />
        <Input
          label="Area of expertise"
          required
          value={expertise}
          onChange={(e) => setExpertise(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g. AI Automation & Workflow Tools"
        />
        <Input
          label="Portfolio link"
          value={portfolioUrl}
          onChange={(e) => setPortfolioUrl(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="GitHub, LinkedIn, or personal site"
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={submitting} className="self-start">
          Submit application
        </Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\cart\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useCartStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function CartPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const items = useCartStore((s) => s.items);
  const coupon = useCartStore((s) => s.coupon);
  const removeItem = useCartStore((s) => s.removeItem);
  const applyCoupon = useCartStore((s) => s.applyCoupon);
  const removeCoupon = useCartStore((s) => s.removeCoupon);
  const subtotal = useCartStore((s) => s.subtotal());
  const discount = useCartStore((s) => s.discount());
  const total = useCartStore((s) => s.total());

  const [couponCode, setCouponCode] = useState("");
  const [couponMessage, setCouponMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  function handleApplyCoupon() {
    const result = applyCoupon(couponCode);
    setCouponMessage(result.message);
  }

  const currency = items[0]?.currency ?? "USD";
  const fmt = (n: number) => new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency }).format(n);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Cart</h1>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Your cart is empty"
          description="Add a course to get started."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 md:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {items.map((item) => (
              <div key={item.courseId} className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4">
                {item.thumbnailUrl && (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img
                    src={item.thumbnailUrl}
                    alt={item.title}
                    className="h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-control object-cover"
                  />
                )}
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:underline">
                    {item.title}
                  </Link>
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{item.instructorName}</p>
                </div>
                <span className="font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: item.currency }).format(item.price)}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label="Remove from cart"
                  onClick={() => removeItem(item.courseId)}
                  className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
                >
                  <X size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <aside className="h-fit rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Order summary</h2>

            {coupon ? (
              <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between rounded-control bg-gold-tint px-3 py-2 text-[13px]">
                <span className="font-semibold text-gold-dark">{coupon.code} applied</span>
                <button type="button" onClick={removeCoupon} className="text-ink-soft hover:text-ink">
                  Remove
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mb-4 flex gap-2">
                <Input
                  placeholder="Coupon code"
                  value={couponCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setCouponCode(e.target.value)}
                  className="flex-1"
                />
                <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleApplyCoupon}>
                  Apply
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
            {couponMessage && <p className="mb-4 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{couponMessage}</p>}

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-line pt-4 text-[14px]">
              <div className="flex justify-between text-ink-soft">
                <span>Subtotal</span>
                <span>{fmt(subtotal)}</span>
              </div>
              {discount > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between text-teal">
                  <span>Discount</span>
                  <span>-{fmt(discount)}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between border-t border-line pt-2 text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">
                <span>Total</span>
                <span>{fmt(total)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <Button size="lg" className="mt-5 w-full" onClick={() => router.push("/checkout")}>
              Proceed to checkout
            </Button>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { BookOpen, Award, Bell } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { useEnrollmentStore, useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, KpiCard } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function LearnerDashboardPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const enrollments = useEnrollmentStore((s) => s.enrollments);
  const notifications = useNotificationStore((s) => s.items);

  const inProgress = enrollments.filter((e) => e.progressPercent > 0 && e.progressPercent < 100);
  const completed = enrollments.filter((e) => e.progressPercent === 100);
  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">
        Welcome back{session ? `, ${session.fullName}` : ""}
      </h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Here is where you left off.</p>

      <div className="mb-10 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <KpiCard label="Enrolled courses" value={String(enrollments.length)} icon={BookOpen} />
        <KpiCard label="Completed" value={String(completed.length)} icon={Award} />
        <KpiCard label="Unread notifications" value={String(unreadCount)} icon={Bell} />
      </div>

      <h2 className="mb-4 font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Continue learning</h2>
      {inProgress.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No courses in progress"
          description="Enroll in a course to see it here."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {inProgress.map((e) => (
            <Link
              key={e.courseId}
              href={`/learn/${e.courseId}/lesson`}
              className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4 transition-colors hover:border-gold-dark/40"
            >
              {e.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={e.thumbnailUrl} alt={e.title} className="h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-control object-cover" />
              )}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <h3 className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{e.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{e.instructorName}</p>
                <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                  <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${e.progressPercent}%` }} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="shrink-0 text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">{e.progressPercent}%</span>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Video, CalendarDays } from "lucide-react";

type Session = {
  id: string;
  instructorName: string;
  topic: string;
  when: string;
  status: "upcoming" | "past";
};

const SESSIONS: Session[] = [
  { id: "s1", instructorName: "Ayesha Khan", topic: "n8n workflow review", when: "Mon, 10:00 AM", status: "upcoming" },
  { id: "s2", instructorName: "Hamza Malik", topic: "API integration Q&A", when: "Last Thu, 9:00 AM", status: "past" }

export default function MyMentorshipSessionsPage() {

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\mentorship\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { CalendarDays, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type MentorSlot = {
  id: string;
  instructorName: string;
  expertise: string;
  rate: string;
  slots: string[];
};

const MENTORS: MentorSlot[] = [
  {
    id: "m1",
    instructorName: "Ayesha Khan",
    expertise: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools",
    rate: "$40 / 30 min",
    slots: ["Mon 10:00 AM", "Wed 2:00 PM", "Fri 11:00 AM"]
  },
  {
    id: "m2",
    instructorName: "Hamza Malik",
    expertise: "AI Integration for Developers",
    rate: "$55 / 45 min",
    slots: ["Tue 4:00 PM", "Thu 9:00 AM"]
  }
];

export default function MentorshipBookingPage() {
  const [selectedMentor, setSelectedMentor] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedSlot, setSelectedSlot] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [booked, setBooked] = useState(false);

  const mentor = useMemo(() => MENTORS.find((m) => m.id === selectedMentor), [selectedMentor]);

  function handleBook() {
    if (!mentor || !selectedSlot) return;
    // NOTE: demo-only booking, no payment/calendar integration wired up yet.
    setBooked(true);
  }

  if (booked && mentor) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg py-16 text-center">
        <EmptyState
          title="Session booked"
          description={`Your session with ${mentor.instructorName} on ${selectedSlot} is confirmed. Check My Mentorship Sessions for the call link.`}
          action={
            <Link href="/mentorship/sessions">
              <Button>View my sessions</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Book mentorship</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">1-on-1 sessions with verified instructors.</p>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        {MENTORS.map((m) => (
          <Card
            key={m.id}
            className={`cursor-pointer p-5 transition-colors ${
              selectedMentor === m.id ? "border-gold-dark/60" : ""
            }`}
            onClick={() => {
              setSelectedMentor(m.id);
              setSelectedSlot(null);
            }}
          >
            <h3 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.instructorName}</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{m.expertise}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.rate}</p>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {mentor && (
        <div className="mt-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
            <CalendarDays size={16} />
            Available slots with {mentor.instructorName}
          </h2>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {mentor.slots.map((slot) => (
              <button
                key={slot}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedSlot(slot)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                  selectedSlot === slot
                    ? "border-gold-dark bg-gold-tint text-ink"
                    : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
                }`}
              >
                <Clock size={13} />
                {slot}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <Button className="mt-6" disabled={!selectedSlot} onClick={handleBook}>
            Confirm booking
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
