FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\my-courses\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useEnrollmentStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, Select } from "@saachi/ui";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "All", value: "all" },
  { label: "In progress", value: "in_progress" },
  { label: "Completed", value: "completed" },
  { label: "Not started", value: "not_started" }
];

export default function MyCoursesPage() {
  const enrollments = useEnrollmentStore((s) => s.enrollments);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");

  const filtered = enrollments.filter((e) => {
    if (status === "in_progress") return e.progressPercent > 0 && e.progressPercent < 100;
    if (status === "completed") return e.progressPercent === 100;
    if (status === "not_started") return e.progressPercent === 0;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">My Courses</h1>
        <Select options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} className="w-48" />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No courses here yet"
          description="Courses you enroll in will show up here."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {filtered.map((e) => (
            <Link
              key={e.courseId}
              href={`/learn/${e.courseId}/lesson`}
              className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card transition-colors hover:border-gold-dark/40"
            >
              {e.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={e.thumbnailUrl} alt={e.title} className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
              )}
              <div className="p-4">
                <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{e.title}</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{e.instructorName}</p>
                <div className="mt-3 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                  <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${e.progressPercent}%` }} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{e.progressPercent}% complete</p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Bell } from "lucide-react";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function NotificationsPage() {
  const items = useNotificationStore((s) => s.items);
  const markRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markRead);
  const markAllRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markAllRead);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Notifications</h1>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={markAllRead}>
          Mark all as read
        </Button>
      </div>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No notifications" description="You're all caught up." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {items.map((n) => (
            <button
              key={n.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => markRead(n.id)}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-start gap-3 rounded-card border border-line p-4 text-left transition-colors",
                n.read ? "bg-card" : "bg-gold-tint/40"
              )}
            >
              <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft">
                <Bell size={14} />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{n.title}</p>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{n.body}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft/60">
                  {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "short" }).format(
                    new Date(n.createdAt)
                  )}
                </p>
              </div>
              {!n.read && <span className="mt-1.5 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gold-dark" />}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\orders\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useOrderStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function OrderSuccessBanner() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const successId = searchParams.get("success");

  if (!successId) return null;

  return (
    <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-teal/30 bg-teal-light px-4 py-3">
      <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-teal" />
      <p className="text-[14px] text-ink">
        Payment successful. Order <span className="font-semibold">{successId}</span> is confirmed - your
        courses are now available in My Courses.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function OrdersTable() {
  const orders = useOrderStore((s) => s.orders);

  if (orders.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState title="No orders yet" description="Your purchase history will show up here." />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
        <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
          <tr>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Order</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Items</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Total</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Date</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {orders.map((order) => (
            <tr key={order.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-medium text-ink">{order.id}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{order.items.map((i) => i.title).join(", ")}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-mono text-ink">
                {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: order.currency }).format(order.total)}
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 capitalize text-ink-soft">{order.status}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">
                {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { dateStyle: "medium" }).format(new Date(order.createdAt))}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function OrdersPage() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Order History</h1>

      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <OrderSuccessBanner />
      </Suspense>

      <OrdersTable />
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Download, ShieldAlert, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsPrivacyPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [cookiePrefs, setCookiePrefs] = useState({ analytics: true, marketing: false });
  const [exportRequested, setExportRequested] = useState(false);

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your privacy settings." />;
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Privacy</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Cookie preferences</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Analytics cookies</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={cookiePrefs.analytics}
              onChange={(e) => setCookiePrefs((p) => ({ ...p, analytics: e.target.checked }))}
              aria-label="Toggle analytics cookies"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Marketing cookies</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={cookiePrefs.marketing}
              onChange={(e) => setCookiePrefs((p) => ({ ...p, marketing: e.target.checked }))}
              aria-label="Toggle marketing cookies"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <Download size={16} />
          Data export
        </h2>
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Request a copy of the personal data associated with your account.
        </p>
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={exportRequested} onClick={() => setExportRequested(true)}>
          {exportRequested ? "Export requested" : "Request data export"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-error/30 bg-error-tint p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <ShieldAlert size={16} />
          Delete account
        </h2>
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Permanently delete your account and all associated data. This cannot be undone.
        </p>
        <Button variant="danger">
          <Trash2 size={14} className="mr-1.5" />
          Request account deletion
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\settings\security\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ShieldCheck, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsSecurityPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [twoFactorEnabled, setTwoFactorEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your security settings." />;
  }

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend auth mutation wired up yet.
    setCurrentPassword("");
    setNewPassword("");
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Security</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <ShieldCheck size={16} />
          Change password
        </h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input
            label="Current password"
            type="password"
            value={currentPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Input
            label="New password"
            type="password"
            value={newPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Button type="submit" className="self-start">
            Update password
          </Button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Smartphone size={16} className="text-ink-soft" />
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Two-factor authentication</p>
              <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Add an extra layer of security to your account.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={twoFactorEnabled}
            onChange={(e) => setTwoFactorEnabled(e.target.checked)}
            aria-label="Toggle two-factor authentication"
            className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Active sessions</h2>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">This device - {session.email}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your settings." />;
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Settings</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Profile</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Full name" defaultValue={session.fullName} />
          <Input label="Email" defaultValue={session.email} type="email" />
          <Button className="self-start">Save changes</Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Session</h2>
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Logged in as {session.email}</p>
        <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => logout.mutate()}>
          Log out
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\wishlist\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useCartStore, useWishlistStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function WishlistPage() {
  const items = useWishlistStore((s) => s.items);
  const removeFromWishlist = useWishlistStore((s) => s.remove);
  const addToCart = useCartStore((s) => s.addItem);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Wishlist</h1>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Your wishlist is empty"
          description="Save courses you're interested in to find them here later."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.courseId}
              className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4"
            >
              {item.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img
                  src={item.thumbnailUrl}
                  alt={item.title}
                  className="h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-control object-cover"
                />
              )}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:underline">
                  {item.title}
                </Link>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{item.instructorName}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: item.currency }).format(item.price)}
              </span>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                onClick={() =>
                  addToCart({
                    courseId: item.courseId,
                    slug: item.slug,
                    title: item.title,
                    thumbnailUrl: item.thumbnailUrl,
                    instructorName: item.instructorName,
                    price: item.price,
                    currency: item.currency
                  })
                }
              >
                Add to cart
              </Button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Remove from wishlist"
                onClick={() => removeFromWishlist(item.courseId)}
                className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
              >
                <X size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\contact\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Button, Input, Textarea, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function ContactPage() {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend yet. Replace with a real Support/Contact API call.
    setSubmitted(true);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-2 font-display text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">Contact us</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[15px] text-ink-soft">
        Questions about courses, instructor applications, or anything else - send us a message.
      </p>

      {submitted ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Message sent"
          description="Thanks for reaching out. We typically respond within 1-2 business days."
        />
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Your name" required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          <Input
            label="Email"
            type="email"
            required
            value={email}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Textarea
            label="Message"
            required
            rows={6}
            value={message}
            onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="How can we help?"
          />
          <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="self-start">
            Send message
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="mt-10 border-t border-line pt-6 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        <p>Support email: support@saachi.ai</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\courses\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useCourseList, type CourseListFilters } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CourseCard, EmptyState, FilterSidebar, Input, Select, Loading, type FilterSidebarValue } from "@saachi/ui";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../../lib/categories";

const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Most popular", value: "popular" },
  { label: "Newest", value: "newest" },
  { label: "Price: low to high", value: "price_asc" },
  { label: "Price: high to low", value: "price_desc" },
  { label: "Highest rated", value: "rating" }
];

const LEVEL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Beginner", value: "beginner" },
  { label: "Intermediate", value: "intermediate" },
  { label: "Advanced", value: "advanced" }
];

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "English", value: "en" },
  { label: "Urdu", value: "ur" }
];

export default function CoursesPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState("popular");
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FilterSidebarValue>({ categories: [], level: undefined, language: undefined });

  const queryFilters: CourseListFilters = useMemo(
    () => ({
      search: search || undefined,
      category: filters.categories[0],
      level: filters.level,
      language: filters.language,
      sort
    }),
    [search, filters, sort]
  );

  const { data, isLoading } = useCourseList(queryFilters);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 md:px-8">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[28px] font-bold text-ink">Course Marketplace</h1>

      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
        <Input
          placeholder="Search courses or instructors..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="md:max-w-sm"
        />
        <Select
          options={SORT_OPTIONS}
          value={sort}
          onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)}
          className="md:w-56"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 md:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
        <FilterSidebar
          categoryOptions={CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id }))}
          levelOptions={LEVEL_OPTIONS}
          languageOptions={LANGUAGE_OPTIONS}
          value={filters}
          onChange={setFilters}
        />

        <div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <Loading label="Loading courses..." />
          ) : !data || data.items.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState title="No courses found" description="Try adjusting your filters or search term." />
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{data.total} courses found</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {data.items.map((course) => (
                  <CourseCard
                    key={course.id}
                    slug={course.slug}
                    title={course.title}
                    thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                    instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                    price={course.price}
                    currency={course.currency}
                    rating={course.rating}
                    ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function InstructorAgreementPage() {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[30px] font-bold text-ink">Instructor Agreement</h1>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">
        <p>
          This Instructor Agreement governs the relationship between Saachi AI and instructors
          who create, publish, and sell courses on the Platform. By submitting a course for
          review or completing the instructor onboarding process, you agree to these terms.
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">1. Content ownership</h2>
        <p>
          Instructors retain ownership of the course content they create. By publishing on
          Saachi AI, you grant the Platform a non-exclusive license to host, stream, and deliver
          that content to enrolled learners.
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">2. Content standards</h2>
        <p>
          Courses must be original work or properly licensed. Plagiarism, unverifiable &quot;get rich
          quick&quot; claims, and use of copyrighted third-party material without rights are strictly
          prohibited and grounds for content removal or account suspension.
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">3. Review &amp; approval</h2>
        <p>
          All courses are reviewed by the Saachi AI moderation team before going live. Admins may
          approve, reject with feedback, or request changes prior to publication.
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">4. Commission &amp; payouts</h2>
        <p>
          Saachi AI retains a platform commission on each course sale, disclosed at the time of
          course submission. Commission rates may vary based on instructor trust tier (New,
          Verified, Top-Rated, Partner). Net earnings are credited to your instructor wallet and
          may be withdrawn according to the payout schedule and method you configure.
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">5. Instructor verification</h2>
        <p>
          Instructors may be required to submit identity or business verification documents and
          portfolio/proof-of-work links. Verification status affects trust tier eligibility and
          may be reviewed periodically.
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">6. Refunds &amp; disputes</h2>
        <p>
          Learner-initiated refunds within the Platform&apos;s refund policy window may reduce your
          earned balance accordingly. Repeated disputes or complaints against your courses may
          trigger a content review.
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">7. Termination</h2>
        <p>
          Saachi AI reserves the right to suspend or terminate an instructor account for violation
          of this Agreement, the Terms &amp; Conditions, or applicable law.
        </p>

        <p className="mt-6 text-[13px] text-ink-soft/70">
          This is placeholder legal text. Replace with content reviewed by legal counsel before
          launch.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

        <p className="text-[15px] text-ink-soft">Buy individual courses, or go unlimited with Saachi AI Pro.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
        {PLANS.map((plan) => (
          <div
            key={plan.name}
            className={`flex flex-col rounded-card border p-6 ${
              plan.highlighted ? "border-gold-dark/50 bg-gold-tint" : "border-line bg-card"
            }`}
          >
            {plan.highlighted && (
              <Badge variant="gold" className="mb-3 self-start">
                Most popular
              </Badge>
            )}
            <h2 className="font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">{plan.name}</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">{plan.price}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{plan.description}</p>

            <ul className="my-6 flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
              {plan.features.map((f) => (
                <li key={f}>- {f}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>

            <Link href={plan.href}>
              <Button className="w-full" variant={plan.highlighted ? "primary" : "secondary"}>
                {plan.cta}
              </Button>
            </Link>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\search\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Suspense, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search as SearchIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useCourseList } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CourseCard, EmptyState, Input, Loading } from "@saachi/ui";

function SearchResults() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const initialQuery = searchParams.get("q") ?? "";
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(initialQuery);

  const { data, isLoading } = useCourseList({ search: initialQuery || undefined });

  function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    router.push(`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 md:px-8">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Search</h1>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mb-8 flex max-w-lg gap-2">
        <Input
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search courses or instructors..."
          className="flex-1"
        />
      </form>

      {!initialQuery ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Search Saachi AI"
          description="Type a course, topic, or instructor name above to get started."
        />
      ) : isLoading ? (
        <Loading label="Searching..." />
      ) : !data || data.items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title={`No results for "${initialQuery}"`}
          description="Try a different search term or browse categories instead."
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <p className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
            <SearchIcon size={14} />
            {data.total} results for &quot;{initialQuery}&quot;
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
            {data.items.map((course) => (
              <CourseCard
                key={course.id}
                slug={course.slug}
                title={course.title}
                thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                price={course.price}
                currency={course.currency}
                rating={course.rating}
                ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SearchPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<Loading label="Loading..." />}>
      <SearchResults />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\_components\SiteHeader.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { ShoppingCart, Heart, Menu, X, LogOut } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, ThemeToggle } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useCartStore, useWishlistStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";

const NAV_LINKS = [
  { href: "/courses", label: "Courses" },
  { href: "/categories/ai-automation", label: "Automation" },
  { href: "/categories/ai-marketing", label: "Marketing" },
  { href: "/about", label: "About" }
];

export function SiteHeader() {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const cartCount = useCartStore((s) => s.items.length);
  const wishlistCount = useWishlistStore((s) => s.items.length);
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-line bg-card/90 backdrop-blur">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 md:px-8">
        <Link href="/" className="font-display text-[20px] font-bold text-ink">
          Saachi<span className="text-gold-dark">AI</span>
        </Link>

        <nav className="hidden items-center gap-6 md:flex">
          {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
            <Link
              key={link.href}
              href={link.href}
              className="text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:text-ink"
            >
              {link.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <ThemeToggle />

          <Link
            href="/wishlist"
            aria-label="Wishlist"
            className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <Heart size={18} />
            {wishlistCount > 0 && (
              <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">
                {wishlistCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/cart"
            aria-label="Cart"
            className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <ShoppingCart size={18} />
            {cartCount > 0 && (
              <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">
                {cartCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>

          {session ? (
            <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
              <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{session.fullName}</span>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="ghost"
                onClick={() => logout.mutate()}
                aria-label="Log out"
              >
                <LogOut size={14} />
              </Button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
              <Link href="/login">
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                  Log in
                </Button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/register">
                <Button size="sm">Sign up</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft md:hidden"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen((v) => !v)}
            aria-label="Toggle menu"
          >
            {mobileOpen ? <X size={20} /> : <Menu size={20} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="border-t border-line bg-card px-4 py-4 md:hidden">
          <nav className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              <Link
                key={link.href}
                href={link.href}
                onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                className="text-[15px] font-medium text-ink"
              >
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
            <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
              {session ? (
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => logout.mutate()} className="w-full">
                  Log out
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Link href="/login" className="flex-1">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="w-full">
                      Log in
                    </Button>
                  </Link>
                  <Link href="/register" className="flex-1">
                    <Button size="sm" className="w-full">
                      Sign up
                    </Button>
                  </Link>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </nav>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Button } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function GlobalError({
  error,
  reset
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error(error);
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-lg flex-col items-center px-4 py-24 text-center md:px-0">
      <span className="font-mono text-[64px] font-bold text-error">500</span>
      <h1 className="mt-2 font-display text-[24px] font-bold text-ink">Something went wrong</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.
      </p>
      <div className="mt-8 flex gap-3">
        <Button onClick={() => reset()}>Try again</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\providers.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";

export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [client] = useState(() => new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: 30_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } }
  }));

  return <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
}

export type CategoryDef = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
};

export const CATEGORIES: CategoryDef[] = [
  { id: "ai-fundamentals", name: "AI Fundamentals & Prompt Engineering", slug: "ai-fundamentals" },
  { id: "ai-marketing", name: "AI for Marketing & Sales", slug: "ai-marketing" },
  { id: "ai-automation", name: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools", slug: "ai-automation" },
  { id: "ai-support", name: "AI Customer Support & Communication", slug: "ai-support" },
  { id: "ai-dev", name: "AI Website & App Development", slug: "ai-dev" },
  { id: "ai-integration", name: "AI Integration for Developers", slug: "ai-integration" },
  { id: "ai-design", name: "AI Design & Creative Tools", slug: "ai-design" },
  { id: "ai-career", name: "AI for Careers & Job Search", slug: "ai-career" }
];

export function getCategoryBySlug(slug: string): CategoryDef | undefined {
  return CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.slug === slug);
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\middleware.ts
================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

const LEARNER_PREFIX = "/dashboard";
const INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX = "/instructor";

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const sessionCookie = request.cookies.get("saachi_session");

  const isProtected =
    pathname.startsWith(LEARNER_PREFIX) || pathname.startsWith(INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX);

  if (isProtected && !sessionCookie) {
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    loginUrl.searchParams.set("redirect", pathname);
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/dashboard/:path*", "/instructor/:path*"]
};

/** @type {import("next").NextConfig} */
const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "https://api.saachi.ai";

const nextConfig = {

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Accordion.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type AccordionItemData = {
  id: string;
  title: ReactNode;
  content: ReactNode;
  meta?: ReactNode;
};

export type AccordionProps = {
  items: AccordionItemData[];
  defaultOpenId?: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function Accordion({ items, defaultOpenId, className }: AccordionProps) {
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(defaultOpenId ?? null);

  return (
    <div className={cn("flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line divide-y divide-line", className)}>
      {items.map((item) => {
        const isOpen = openId === item.id;
        return (
          <div key={item.id} className="bg-card">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpenId(isOpen ? null : item.id)}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4 text-left"
              aria-expanded={isOpen}
            >
              <div className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-3">
                <span className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{item.title}</span>
                {item.meta}
              </div>
              <ChevronDown
                size={18}
                className={cn(
                  "shrink-0 text-ink-soft transition-transform duration-200 ease-out",
                  isOpen && "rotate-180 text-gold-dark"
                )}
              />
            </button>
            <div
              className={cn(
                "grid transition-all duration-200 ease-out",
                isOpen ? "grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100" : "grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0"
              )}
            >
              <div className="overflow-hidden">
                <div className="px-5 pb-4 text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{item.content}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

type BadgeVariant = "default" | "success" | "warning" | "error" | "gold";

export type BadgeProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> & {
  variant?: BadgeVariant;
};

const variantStyles: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
  default: "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft",
  success: "bg-teal-light text-teal",
  warning: "bg-gold-tint text-gold-dark",
  error: "bg-error-tint text-error",
  gold: "bg-gold text-ink"
};

export function Badge({ className, variant = "default", children, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold",
        variantStyles[variant],
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

export type ButtonProps = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
};

const variantStyles: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  primary: "bg-gold text-ink hover:bg-gold-dark",
  secondary: "border border-line-strong bg-card text-ink hover:border-ink",
  ghost: "bg-transparent text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim",
  danger: "bg-error text-white hover:bg-[#a53931]"
};

const sizeStyles: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  sm: "h-9 px-4 text-sm",
  md: "h-11 px-5 text-[15px]",
  lg: "h-[52px] px-7 text-[16px]"
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant = "primary", size = "md", loading, disabled, children, ...props }, ref) => {
    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        disabled={disabled || loading}
        className={cn(
          "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]",
          "transition-colors duration-150 ease-out disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none",
          variantStyles[variant],
          sizeStyles[size],
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {loading ? "Workingâ€¦" : children}
      </button>
    );
  }
);
Button.displayName = "Button";

import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type CheckboxProps = Omit<InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, "type"> & {
  label?: string;
};

export const Checkbox = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, CheckboxProps>(
  ({ className, label, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const checkboxId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <label htmlFor={checkboxId} className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 select-none">
        <span className="relative inline-flex h-[18px] w-[18px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
          <input
            ref={ref}
            type="checkbox"
            id={checkboxId}
            className={cn("peer absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0", className)}
            {...props}
          />
          <span className="flex h-[18px] w-[18px] items-center justify-center rounded-[5px] border border-line-strong bg-card transition-colors duration-150 ease-out peer-checked:border-gold peer-checked:bg-gold peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-gold/40">
            <Check size={13} strokeWidth={3} className="text-ink opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100" />
          </span>
        </span>
        {label && <span className="text-[14px] text-ink">{label}</span>}
      </label>
    );
  }
);
Checkbox.displayName = "Checkbox";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Star } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type CourseCardProps = {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency?: string;
  rating: number;
  ratingCount: number;
  className?: string;
};

export function CourseCard({
  slug,
  title,
  thumbnailUrl,
  instructorName,
  price,
  currency = "USD",
  rating,
  ratingCount,
  className
}: CourseCardProps) {
  const formattedPrice = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    minimumFractionDigits: price % 1 === 0 ? 0 : 2
  }).format(price);

  return (
    <Link
      href={`/courses/${slug}`}
      className={cn(
        "group flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card",
        "transition-all duration-200 ease-out hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-gold-dark/40",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="relative aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden bg-paper-dim">
        {thumbnailUrl ? (
          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
          <img
            src={thumbnailUrl}
            alt={title}
            className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.02]"
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[13px] text-ink-soft/60">
            No preview
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 p-4">
        <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold leading-snug text-ink">{title}</h3>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{instructorName}</p>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px]">
          <Star size={14} className="fill-gold text-gold" />
          <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{rating.toFixed(1)}</span>
          <span className="text-ink-soft/60">({ratingCount.toLocaleString()})</span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-auto pt-2 font-mono text-[17px] font-semibold text-ink">{formattedPrice}</div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\DataTable.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { ChevronUp, ChevronDown, ChevronsUpDown } from "lucide-react";
import {
  flexRender,
  getCoreRowModel,
  getPaginationRowModel,
  getSortedRowModel,
  useReactTable,
  type ColumnDef,
  type SortingState
} from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useState } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";
import { ShimmerTable } from "./Shimmer";
import { EmptyState } from "./EmptyState";
import { Button } from "./Button";

export type DataTableProps<T> = {
  columns: ColumnDef<T, any>[];
  data: T[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
  emptyTitle?: string;
  emptyDescription?: string;
  pageSize?: number;
  toolbar?: ReactNode;
  onRowClick?: (row: T) => void;
};

export function DataTable<T>({
  columns,
  data,
  isLoading,
  emptyTitle = "Nothing here yet",
  emptyDescription,
  pageSize = 10,
  toolbar,
  onRowClick
}: DataTableProps<T>) {
  const [sorting, setSorting] = useState<SortingState>([]);

  const table = useReactTable({
    data,
    columns,
    state: { sorting },
    onSortingChange: setSorting,
    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
    getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel(),
    getPaginationRowModel: getPaginationRowModel(),
    initialState: { pagination: { pageSize } }
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {toolbar}
        <ShimmerTable rows={6} columns={columns.length} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {toolbar}
        <EmptyState title={emptyTitle} description={emptyDescription} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {toolbar}
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
            <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
              {table.getHeaderGroups().map((headerGroup) => (
                <tr key={headerGroup.id}>
                  {headerGroup.headers.map((header) => {
                    const sortState = header.column.getIsSorted();
                    return (
                      <th key={header.id} className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">
                        {header.isPlaceholder ? null : (
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={header.column.getToggleSortingHandler()}
                            className={cn(
                              "flex items-center gap-1.5",
                              header.column.getCanSort() ? "cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-ink" : "cursor-default"
                            )}
                          >
                            {flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())}
                            {header.column.getCanSort() &&
                              (sortState === "asc" ? (
                                <ChevronUp size={13} />
                              ) : sortState === "desc" ? (
                                <ChevronDown size={13} />
                              ) : (
                                <ChevronsUpDown size={13} className="opacity-40" />
                              ))}
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </th>
                    );
                  })}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => (
                <tr
                  key={row.id}
                  onClick={() => onRowClick?.(row.original)}
                  className={cn(
                    "border-b border-line last:border-0",
                    onRowClick && "cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                  )}
                >
                  {row.getVisibleCells().map((cell) => (
                    <td key={cell.id} className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3.5 text-ink">
                      {flexRender(cell.column.columnDef.cell, cell.getContext())}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {table.getPageCount() > 1 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1">
          <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            Page {table.getState().pagination.pageIndex + 1} of {table.getPageCount()}
          </span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => table.previousPage()} disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}>
              Previous
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => table.nextPage()} disabled={!table.getCanNextPage()}>
              Next
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Inbox } from "lucide-react";

export type EmptyStateProps = {

  description?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
};

export function EmptyState({ icon, title, description, action, className }: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-dashed border-line-strong px-8 py-16 text-center",
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold-tint text-gold-dark">
        {icon ?? <Inbox size={22} />}
      </div>
      <h3 className="font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h3>
      {description && <p className="max-w-[420px] text-[14px] text-ink-soft">{description}</p>}
      {action && <div className="mt-2">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\FilterSidebar.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Checkbox } from "./Checkbox";
import { Select } from "./Select";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type FilterSidebarValue = {
  categories: string[];
  level?: string;
  language?: string;
};

export type FilterSidebarProps = {
  categoryOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  levelOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  languageOptions: { label: string; value: string }[];
  value: FilterSidebarValue;
  onChange: (next: FilterSidebarValue) => void;
  className?: string;
};

export function FilterSidebar({
  categoryOptions,
  levelOptions,
  languageOptions,
  value,
  onChange,
  className
}: FilterSidebarProps) {
  const toggleCategory = (cat: string) => {
    const isActive = value.categories.includes(cat);
    const nextCategories = isActive
      ? value.categories.filter((c) => c !== cat)
      : [...value.categories, cat];
    onChange({ ...value, categories: nextCategories });
  };

  return (
    <aside className={cn("flex w-full flex-col gap-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6", className)}>
      <div>
        <h4 className="mb-3 text-[13px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft/70">
          Category
        </h4>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          {categoryOptions.map((opt) => (
            <Checkbox
              key={opt.value}
              label={opt.label}
              checked={value.categories.includes(opt.value)}
              onChange={() => toggleCategory(opt.value)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <Select
        label="Skill level"
        placeholder="All levels"
        options={levelOptions}
        value={value.level ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, level: e.target.value || undefined })}
      />

      <Select
        label="Language"
        placeholder="All languages"
        options={languageOptions}
        value={value.language ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, language: e.target.value || undefined })}
      />

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onChange({ categories: [], level: undefined, language: undefined })}
        className="text-left text-[13px] font-medium text-gold-dark hover:underline"
      >
        Clear all filters
      </button>
    </aside>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Input.tsx
================================================================================
import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type InputProps = InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
};

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const inputId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={inputId}
          className={cn(
            "h-11 w-full rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 text-[15px] text-ink",
            "placeholder:text-ink-soft/50 outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
            "focus:border-gold",
            error && "border-error focus:border-error",
            className
          )}
          aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
          {...props}
        />
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Input.displayName = "Input";

};

    <div className={cn("rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5", className)}>
      <div className="mb-4 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink">

            )}

            {delta.value}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Loading.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Lottie from "lottie-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type LoadingProps = {
  size?: number;
  label?: string;
  fullScreen?: boolean;
  className?: string;
};

export function Loading({ size = 120, label, fullScreen = false, className }: LoadingProps) {
  const [animationData, setAnimationData] = useState<object | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch("/animations/loading.json")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (!cancelled) setAnimationData(data);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // Silently ignore Ã¢â‚¬â€ falls back to just showing the label below.
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3",
        fullScreen ? "min-h-[60vh] w-full" : "py-10",
        className
      )}
      role="status"
      aria-live="polite"
    >
      {animationData ? (
        <Lottie animationData={animationData} loop autoplay style={{ width: size, height: size }} />
      ) : (
        <div
          className="animate-pulse rounded-full border-2 border-line-strong border-t-gold"
          style={{ width: size / 2, height: size / 2 }}
        />
      )}
      {label && <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{label}</p>}
      <span className="sr-only">Loading</span>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\Modal.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { type ReactNode, useEffect } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ModalProps = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  size?: "md" | "lg";
};

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, children, footer, size = "md" }: ModalProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div
        className={cn(
          "relative w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6",
          size === "lg" ? "max-w-[720px]" : "max-w-[520px]"
        )}
      >
        {title && (
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h2>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Close"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:bg-paper-dim"
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        <div>{children}</div>
        {footer && <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">{footer}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Star, BadgeCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ReviewCardProps = {
  authorName: string;
  authorAvatarUrl?: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  createdAt: string;
  verifiedPurchase?: boolean;
  className?: string;
};

export function ReviewCard({
  authorName,
  authorAvatarUrl,
  rating,
  comment,
  createdAt,
  verifiedPurchase,
  className
}: ReviewCardProps) {
  const formattedDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric"
  }).format(new Date(createdAt));

  return (
    <div className={cn("rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6", className)}>
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1">
        {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
          <Star key={i} size={15} className={i < Math.round(rating) ? "fill-gold text-gold" : "text-line-strong"} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <p className="mb-4 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-ink">{comment}</p>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-teal-light text-[13px] font-semibold text-teal">
          {authorAvatarUrl ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img src={authorAvatarUrl} alt={authorName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
          ) : (
            authorName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()
          )}
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{authorName}</span>
            {verifiedPurchase && <BadgeCheck size={14} className="text-teal" />}
          </div>
          <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft/60">{formattedDate}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type SelectHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type SelectOption = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
};

export type SelectProps = SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
  options: SelectOption[];
  placeholder?: string;
};

export const Select = forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, SelectProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, options, placeholder, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const selectId = id ?? props.name;

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={selectId} className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <div className="relative">
          <select
            ref={ref}
            id={selectId}
            className={cn(
              "h-11 w-full appearance-none rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 pr-10 text-[15px] text-ink",
              "outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out focus:border-gold",
              error && "border-error focus:border-error",
              className
            )}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
            {...props}
          >
            {placeholder && (
              <option value="" disabled>
                {placeholder}
              </option>
            )}
            {options.map((opt) => (
              <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                {opt.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <ChevronDown
            size={18}
            className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft"
          />
        </div>
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-error">{error}</p>
        ) : helperText ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{helperText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Select.displayName = "Select";

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export type ShimmerProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>;

/** Base shimmer block - grayscale sweep, works in light + dark theme. */
export function Shimmer({ className, ...props }: ShimmerProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "relative overflow-hidden rounded-control bg-paper-dim",
        "before:absolute before:inset-0 before:-translate-x-full",
        "before:bg-gradient-to-r before:from-transparent before:via-white/60 before:to-transparent",
        "before:animate-shimmer dark:before:via-white/10",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export function ShimmerText({ lines = 3, lastLineWidth = "60%" }: { lines?: number; lastLineWidth?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {Array.from({ length: lines }).map((_, i) => (
        <Shimmer key={i} className="h-3.5" style={{ width: i === lines - 1 ? lastLineWidth : "100%" }} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerKpiCard() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
      <Shimmer className="mb-4 h-9 w-9 rounded-full" />
      <Shimmer className="mb-2 h-6 w-20" />
      <Shimmer className="h-3.5 w-28" />
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerTableRow({ columns = 5 }: { columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b border-line px-4 py-3.5">
      {Array.from({ length: columns }).map((_, i) => (
        <Shimmer key={i} className="h-3.5 flex-1" />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerTable({ rows = 6, columns = 5 }: { rows?: number; columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      {Array.from({ length: rows }).map((_, i) => (
        <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={columns} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ShimmerChart() {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
      <Shimmer className="mb-6 h-4 w-32" />
      <Shimmer className="h-[220px] w-full" />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\components\ThemeToggle.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Sun, Moon } from "lucide-react";
import { useThemeStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { cn } from "../cn";

export function ThemeToggle({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useThemeStore();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => setMounted(true), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return;
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  }, [theme, mounted]);

  if (!mounted) {
    return <div className={cn("h-9 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-control", className)} />;
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      aria-label={theme === "light" ? "Switch to dark mode" : "Switch to light mode"}
      className={cn(
        "flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim",
        className
      )}
    >
      {theme === "light" ? <Moon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <Sun size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
    </button>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: packages\ui\src\Button.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { forwardRef, useState } from "react";
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, CSSProperties } from "react";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

type ButtonProps = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
};

const SIZE_CLASSES: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  sm: "h-8 px-3 text-[12.5px]",
  md: "h-9 px-4 text-[13px]",
  lg: "h-11 px-5 text-[14px]"
};

const VARIANT_STYLE: Record<ButtonVariant, { bg: string; hoverBg: string; text: string; border: string }> = {
  primary: {
    bg: "var(--color-ink)",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-ink-soft)",
    text: "var(--color-paper)",
    border: "var(--color-ink)"
  },
  secondary: {
    bg: "var(--color-card)",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-paper-dim)",
    text: "var(--color-ink)",
    border: "var(--color-line-strong)"
  },
  ghost: {
    bg: "transparent",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-paper-dim)",
    text: "var(--color-ink-soft)",
    border: "transparent"
  },
  danger: {
    bg: "var(--color-error)",
    hoverBg: "var(--color-error)",
    text: "var(--color-paper)",
    border: "var(--color-error)"
  }
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(function Button(
  {
    variant = "primary",
    size = "md",
    className = "",
    type = "button",
    style,
    disabled,
    loading = false,
    children,
    onMouseDown,
    onMouseUp,
    ...props
  },
  ref
) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(false);
  const [pressed, setPressed] = useState(false);

  const v = VARIANT_STYLE[variant];
  const isDisabled = disabled || loading;

  const dynamicStyle: CSSProperties = {
    backgroundColor: isDisabled ? v.bg : hover ? v.hoverBg : v.bg,
    color: v.text,
    borderColor: v.border,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderStyle: "solid",
    opacity: isDisabled ? 0.6 : variant === "danger" && hover ? 0.9 : 1,
    transform: !isDisabled && hover ? (pressed ? "translateY(0) scale(0.97)" : "translateY(-1px)") : "translateY(0)",
    boxShadow: !isDisabled && hover ? "0 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.18)" : "none",
    transition: "background-color 150ms ease-out, transform 120ms ease-out, box-shadow 150ms ease-out, opacity 150ms ease-out",
    cursor: isDisabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
    ...style
  };

  const classes = [
    "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-control font-medium whitespace-nowrap outline-none",
    "focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-ink",
    SIZE_CLASSES[size],
    className
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(" ");

  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      disabled={isDisabled}
      aria-busy={loading}
      className={classes}
      style={dynamicStyle}
      onMouseEnter={() => setHover(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => {
        setHover(false);
        setPressed(false);
      }}
      onMouseDown={(e) => {
        setPressed(true);
        onMouseDown?.(e);
      }}
      onMouseUp={(e) => {
        setPressed(false);
        onMouseUp?.(e);
      }}
      {...props}
    >
      {loading ? "Working..." : children}
    </button>
  );
});

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\HelpTooltip.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { HelpCircle } from "lucide-react";

type HelpTooltipProps = {
  text: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function HelpTooltip({ text, className = "" }: HelpTooltipProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    }
    if (open) document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className={`relative inline-flex ${className}`} ref={ref}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          setOpen((v) => !v);
        }}
        onMouseEnter={() => setOpen(true)}
        onMouseLeave={() => setOpen(false)}
        className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
        aria-label="Help"
      >
        <HelpCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="absolute left-0 top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-20 w-[260px] rounded-control border border-line bg-card p-3 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft shadow-lg"
        >
          {text}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: packages\ui\src\RefreshButton.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { RotateCw } from "lucide-react";

type RefreshButtonProps = {
  className?: string;
  title?: string;
};

export function RefreshButton({ className = "", title = "Refresh" }: RefreshButtonProps) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [spinning, setSpinning] = useState(false);

  const handleRefresh = async () => {
    if (spinning) return;
    setSpinning(true);
    await queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    window.setTimeout(() => setSpinning(false), 500);
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleRefresh}
      title={title}
      aria-label={title}
      className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-line-strong hover:text-ink ${className}`}
    >
      <RotateCw size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className={spinning ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
    </button>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: packages\ui\src\RowActionsMenu.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { MoreVertical } from "lucide-react";

export type RowAction = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  onClick: () => void;
  tone?: "default" | "danger";
  disabled?: boolean;
};

type RowActionsMenuProps = {
  actions: RowAction[];
  ariaLabel?: string;
};

export function RowActionsMenu({ actions, ariaLabel = "Row actions" }: RowActionsMenuProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    }
    if (open) {
      document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    }
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block" ref={ref}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          setOpen((v) => !v);
        }}
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
      >
        <MoreVertical size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+4px)] z-30 w-[190px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg"
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col p-1.5">
            {actions.map((action) => (
              <button
                key={action.id}
                type="button"
                disabled={action.disabled}
                onClick={() => {
                  action.onClick();
                  setOpen(false);
                }}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-left text-[13px] transition-colors duration-100 ease-out disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 ${
                  action.tone === "danger"
                    ? "text-error hover:bg-error-tint"
                    : "text-ink hover:bg-paper-dim"
                }`}
              >
                {action.icon}
                {action.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: packages\ui\src\Switch.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

type SwitchProps = {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  label?: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function Switch({ checked, onChange, disabled = false, label, className = "" }: SwitchProps) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(false);

  const trackStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    position: "relative",
    display: "inline-flex",
    alignItems: "center",
    width: 44,
    height: 24,
    borderRadius: 999,
    border: "1px solid",
    borderColor: checked ? "var(--color-ink)" : "var(--color-line-strong)",
    backgroundColor: checked ? "var(--color-ink)" : "var(--color-paper-dim)",
    opacity: disabled ? 0.4 : hover ? 0.85 : 1,
    cursor: disabled ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
    transition: "background-color 180ms ease-out, border-color 180ms ease-out, opacity 150ms ease-out",
    outline: "none",
    padding: 0,
    flexShrink: 0
  };

  const knobStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 2,
    left: checked ? 22 : 2,
    width: 18,
    height: 18,
    borderRadius: "50%",
    backgroundColor: "var(--color-paper)",
    boxShadow: "0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
    transition: "left 180ms ease-out"
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={onChange}
      onMouseEnter={() => setHover(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => setHover(false)}
      style={trackStyle}
      className={className}
    >
      <span style={knobStyle} />
    </button>
  );
}
