FILE: packages\utils\src\formatting\currency.ts
================================================================================
const CURRENCY_LOCALE: Record<string, string> = {
  USD: "en-US",
  PKR: "en-PK",
  GBP: "en-GB"
};

export function formatCurrency(amount: number, currency: string = "USD"): string {
  const locale = CURRENCY_LOCALE[currency] ?? "en-US";
  return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    minimumFractionDigits: 2
  }).format(amount);
}

export function formatCompactNumber(value: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    notation: "compact",
    maximumFractionDigits: 1
  }).format(value);
}



================================================================================
FILE: packages\utils\src\formatting\date.ts
================================================================================
export function formatDate(input: string | Date, locale: string = "en-US"): string {
  const date = typeof input === "string" ? new Date(input) : input;
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric"
  }).format(date);
}

export function formatRelativeTime(input: string | Date): string {
  const date = typeof input === "string" ? new Date(input) : input;
  const diffMs = date.getTime() - Date.now();
  const diffMinutes = Math.round(diffMs / 60000);

  const rtf = new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat("en", { numeric: "auto" });

  if (Math.abs(diffMinutes) < 60) return rtf.format(diffMinutes, "minute");
  const diffHours = Math.round(diffMinutes / 60);
  if (Math.abs(diffHours) < 24) return rtf.format(diffHours, "hour");
  const diffDays = Math.round(diffHours / 24);
  return rtf.format(diffDays, "day");
}

export function formatDuration(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const hours = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}h ${minutes}m`;
  return `${minutes}m`;
}

import { z } from "zod";

export const loginSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email("Enter a valid email address"),
  password: z.string().min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters")
});
export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;

export const registerSchema = z
  .object({
    fullName: z.string().min(2, "Full name is required"),
    email: z.string().email("Enter a valid email address"),
    password: z.string().min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters"),
    confirmPassword: z.string(),
    role: z.enum(["learner", "instructor"]).default("learner")
  })
  .refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
    message: "Passwords do not match",
    path: ["confirmPassword"]
  });
export type RegisterInput = z.infer<typeof registerSchema>;

export const forgotPasswordSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email("Enter a valid email address")
});
export type ForgotPasswordInput = z.infer<typeof forgotPasswordSchema>;

export const resetPasswordSchema = z
  .object({
    token: z.string().min(1),
    password: z.string().min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters"),
    confirmPassword: z.string()
  })
  .refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
    message: "Passwords do not match",
    path: ["confirmPassword"]
  });
export type ResetPasswordInput = z.infer<typeof resetPasswordSchema>;

export const twoFactorSchema = z.object({
  code: z.string().length(6, "Enter the 6-digit code")
});
export type TwoFactorInput = z.infer<typeof twoFactorSchema>;

import { z } from "zod";

export const courseBasicsSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(5, "Title must be at least 5 characters").max(120),
  subtitle: z.string().max(180).optional(),
  categoryId: z.string().min(1, "Select a category"),
  tags: z.array(z.string()).max(10).default([]),
  language: z.string().min(1, "Select a language"),
  level: z.enum(["beginner", "intermediate", "advanced"])
});
export type CourseBasicsInput = z.infer<typeof courseBasicsSchema>;

export const coursePricingSchema = z.object({
  price: z.number().min(0, "Price cannot be negative"),
  currency: z.enum(["PKR", "USD", "GBP"]).default("USD"),
  subscriptionIncluded: z.boolean().default(false),
  couponEligible: z.boolean().default(true)
});
export type CoursePricingInput = z.infer<typeof coursePricingSchema>;

export const lessonSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(3, "Lesson title is required"),
  type: z.enum(["video", "text", "quiz", "resource"]),
  content: z.string().optional(),
  durationSeconds: z.number().int().min(0).optional()
});
export type LessonInput = z.infer<typeof lessonSchema>;

export const moduleSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(3, "Module title is required"),
  lessons: z.array(lessonSchema).default([])
});
export type ModuleInput = z.infer<typeof moduleSchema>;

export const curriculumSchema = z.object({
  modules: z.array(moduleSchema).min(1, "Add at least one module")
});
export type CurriculumInput = z.infer<typeof curriculumSchema>;

FILE: packages\store\src\useCartStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type CartItem = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
};

export type CouponState = {
  code: string;
  percentOff: number;
} | null;

// NOTE: demo-only coupon codes, validated client-side. Replace with a real
// Coupons API call once the backend (Doc 3) is available.
const KNOWN_COUPONS: Record<string, number> = {
  SAACHI10: 10,
  SAACHI25: 25
};

type CartState = {
  items: CartItem[];
  coupon: CouponState;
  addItem: (item: CartItem) => void;
  removeItem: (courseId: string) => void;
  clear: () => void;
  applyCoupon: (code: string) => { success: boolean; message: string };
  removeCoupon: () => void;
  isInCart: (courseId: string) => boolean;
  subtotal: () => number;
  discount: () => number;
  total: () => number;
};

export const useCartStore = create<CartState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      items: [],
      coupon: null,

      addItem: (item) => {
        const exists = get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === item.courseId);
        if (exists) return;
        set({ items: [...get().items, item] });
      },

      removeItem: (courseId) => {
        set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== courseId) });
      },

      clear: () => set({ items: [], coupon: null }),

      applyCoupon: (code) => {
        const normalized = code.trim().toUpperCase();
        const percentOff = KNOWN_COUPONS[normalized];
        if (!percentOff) {
          return { success: false, message: "That coupon code isn't valid." };
        }
        set({ coupon: { code: normalized, percentOff } });
        return { success: true, message: `${percentOff}% discount applied.` };
      },

      removeCoupon: () => set({ coupon: null }),

      isInCart: (courseId) => get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === courseId),

      subtotal: () => get().items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.price, 0),

      discount: () => {
        const coupon = get().coupon;
        if (!coupon) return 0;
        return Math.round(get().subtotal() * (coupon.percentOff / 100) * 100) / 100;
      },

      total: () => Math.max(0, get().subtotal() - get().discount())
    }),
    { name: "saachi-cart" }
  )
);

FILE: packages\store\src\useCommunityStore.ts
================================================================================
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type CommunityAnswer = {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  isInstructor: boolean;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  upvotes: number;
};

export type CommunityQuestion = {
  id: string;
  courseId: string;
  authorName: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  upvotes: number;
  answers: CommunityAnswer[];
};

const SEED_QUESTIONS: CommunityQuestion[] = [
  {
    id: "q_1",
    courseId: "crs_001",
    authorName: "Hina Malik",
    body: "For few-shot prompting, how many examples is usually enough before it stops improving results?",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 30).toISOString(),
    upvotes: 6,
    answers: [
      {
        id: "a_1",
        authorName: "Sana Rizvi",
        isInstructor: true,
        body: "Usually 3-5 well-chosen examples get you most of the benefit - past that you're often better off improving instructions instead.",
        createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 20).toISOString(),
        upvotes: 9
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    id: "q_2",
    courseId: "crs_001",
    authorName: "Bilal Ahmed",
    body: "Does the prompt library template work with Claude and Gemini too, or is it ChatGPT-specific?",
    createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000 * 60 * 60 * 10).toISOString(),
    upvotes: 2,
    answers: []
  }
];

type CommunityState = {
  questions: CommunityQuestion[];
  addQuestion: (courseId: string, authorName: string, body: string) => void;
  addAnswer: (questionId: string, authorName: string, body: string) => void;
  upvoteQuestion: (questionId: string) => void;
};

function genId(prefix: string) {
  return `${prefix}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;
}

export const useCommunityStore = create<CommunityState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      questions: SEED_QUESTIONS,

      addQuestion: (courseId, authorName, body) => {
        const newQuestion: CommunityQuestion = {
          id: genId("q"),
          courseId,
          authorName,
          body,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          upvotes: 0,
          answers: []
        };
        set({ questions: [newQuestion, ...get().questions] });
      },

      addAnswer: (questionId, authorName, body) => {
        set({
          questions: get().questions.map((q) =>
            q.id === questionId
              ? {
                  ...q,
                  answers: [
                    ...q.answers,
                    {
                      id: genId("a"),
                      authorName,
                      isInstructor: false,
                      body,
                      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                      upvotes: 0
                    }
                  ]
                }
              : q
          )
        });
      },

      upvoteQuestion: (questionId) => {
        set({
          questions: get().questions.map((q) =>
            q.id === questionId ? { ...q, upvotes: q.upvotes + 1 } : q
          )
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-community" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type TeamMember = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  status: "invited" | "active";
  assignedCourseIds: string[];
  completedCourseIds: string[];
};

export type CorporateAccount = {
  companyName: string;
  seatsPurchased: number;
  seatsUsed: number;
};

const SEED_TEAM: TeamMember[] = [
  {
    id: "tm_1",
    name: "Zainab Rehman",
    email: "zainab@acmeagency.com",
    status: "active",
    assignedCourseIds: ["crs_001", "crs_002"],
    completedCourseIds: ["crs_001"]
  },
  {
    id: "tm_2",
    name: "Ali Raza",
    email: "ali@acmeagency.com",
    status: "active",
    assignedCourseIds: ["crs_003"],
    completedCourseIds: []
  },
  {
    id: "tm_3",
    name: "Mahnoor Fatima",
    email: "mahnoor@acmeagency.com",
    status: "invited",
    assignedCourseIds: [],
    completedCourseIds: []
  }
];

type CorporateState = {
  account: CorporateAccount;
  team: TeamMember[];
  inviteMember: (name: string, email: string) => void;
  removeMember: (id: string) => void;
  assignCourse: (memberId: string, courseId: string) => void;
  purchaseSeats: (count: number) => void;
};

export const useCorporateStore = create<CorporateState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      account: { companyName: "Acme Agency", seatsPurchased: 10, seatsUsed: 3 },
      team: SEED_TEAM,

      inviteMember: (name, email) => {
        const newMember: TeamMember = {
          id: `tm_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
          name,
          email,
          status: "invited",
          assignedCourseIds: [],
          completedCourseIds: []
        };
        set({
          team: [...get().team, newMember],
          account: { ...get().account, seatsUsed: get().account.seatsUsed + 1 }
        });
      },

      removeMember: (id) => {
        set({
          team: get().team.filter((m) => m.id !== id),
          account: { ...get().account, seatsUsed: Math.max(0, get().account.seatsUsed - 1) }
        });
      },

      assignCourse: (memberId, courseId) => {
        set({
          team: get().team.map((m) =>
            m.id === memberId && !m.assignedCourseIds.includes(courseId)
              ? { ...m, assignedCourseIds: [...m.assignedCourseIds, courseId] }
              : m
          )
        });
      },

      purchaseSeats: (count) => {
        set({ account: { ...get().account, seatsPurchased: get().account.seatsPurchased + count } });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-corporate" }
  )
);

FILE: packages\store\src\useEnrollmentStore.ts

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type EnrollmentRecord = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  progressPercent: number;
  purchasedAt: string;
  completedLessonIds: string[];
};

type EnrollmentState = {
  enrollments: EnrollmentRecord[];
  isEnrolled: (courseId: string) => boolean;
  enroll: (records: Omit<EnrollmentRecord, "progressPercent" | "purchasedAt" | "completedLessonIds">[]) => void;
  markLessonComplete: (courseId: string, lessonId: string, totalLessons: number) => void;
  getEnrollment: (courseId: string) => EnrollmentRecord | undefined;
};

export const useEnrollmentStore = create<EnrollmentState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      enrollments: [],

      isEnrolled: (courseId) => get().enrollments.some((e) => e.courseId === courseId),

      getEnrollment: (courseId) => get().enrollments.find((e) => e.courseId === courseId),

      enroll: (records) => {
        const now = new Date().toISOString();
        const existingIds = new Set(get().enrollments.map((e) => e.courseId));
        const fresh = records
          .filter((r) => !existingIds.has(r.courseId))
          .map((r) => ({ ...r, progressPercent: 0, purchasedAt: now, completedLessonIds: [] as string[] }));
        if (fresh.length === 0) return;
        set({ enrollments: [...get().enrollments, ...fresh] });
      },

      markLessonComplete: (courseId, lessonId, totalLessons) => {
        set({
          enrollments: get().enrollments.map((e) => {
            if (e.courseId !== courseId) return e;
            const completedLessonIds = e.completedLessonIds.includes(lessonId)
              ? e.completedLessonIds
              : [...e.completedLessonIds, lessonId];
            const progressPercent =
              totalLessons > 0 ? Math.round((completedLessonIds.length / totalLessons) * 100) : 0;
            return { ...e, completedLessonIds, progressPercent };
          })
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-enrollments" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type InstructorCourseLesson = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  type: "video" | "text" | "quiz" | "resource";
};

export type InstructorCourseModule = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  lessons: InstructorCourseLesson[];
};

export type InstructorCourse = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  categoryId: string;
  language: string;
  level: "beginner" | "intermediate" | "advanced";
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  status: "draft" | "pending_review" | "published";
  modules: InstructorCourseModule[];
  createdAt: string;
  enrollmentCount: number;
  revenue: number;
  rating: number;
};

type InstructorCourseState = {
  courses: InstructorCourse[];
  addCourse: (course: InstructorCourse) => void;
  updateCourse: (id: string, patch: Partial<InstructorCourse>) => void;
  removeCourse: (id: string) => void;
};

export const useInstructorCourseStore = create<InstructorCourseState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      courses: [],
      addCourse: (course) => set({ courses: [course, ...get().courses] }),
      updateCourse: (id, patch) =>
        set({ courses: get().courses.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, ...patch } : c)) }),
      removeCourse: (id) => set({ courses: get().courses.filter((c) => c.id !== id) })
    }),
    { name: "saachi-instructor-courses" }
  )
);

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type ActivePromotion = {
  courseId: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  tier: "bronze" | "silver" | "gold";
  purchasedAt: string;
  expiresAt: string;
};

type PromotionState = {
  active: ActivePromotion[];
  purchase: (courseId: string, courseTitle: string, tier: ActivePromotion["tier"]) => void;
};

export const usePromotionStore = create<PromotionState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      active: [],
      purchase: (courseId, courseTitle, tier) => {
        const now = new Date();
        const expires = new Date(now.getTime() + 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 14);
        set({
          active: [
            ...get().active.filter((p) => p.courseId !== courseId),
            { courseId, courseTitle, tier, purchasedAt: now.toISOString(), expiresAt: expires.toISOString() }
          ]
        });
      }
    }),
    { name: "saachi-promotions" }
  )
);

FILE: packages\store\src\useWishlistStore.ts

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

export type WishlistItem = {
  courseId: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  instructorName: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  rating: number;
  ratingCount: number;
};

type WishlistState = {
  items: WishlistItem[];
  toggle: (item: WishlistItem) => void;
  remove: (courseId: string) => void;
  isWishlisted: (courseId: string) => boolean;
};

export const useWishlistStore = create<WishlistState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      items: [],

      toggle: (item) => {
        const exists = get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === item.courseId);
        if (exists) {
          set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== item.courseId) });
        } else {
          set({ items: [...get().items, item] });
        }
      },

      remove: (courseId) => {
        set({ items: get().items.filter((i) => i.courseId !== courseId) });
      },

      isWishlisted: (courseId) => get().items.some((i) => i.courseId === courseId)
    }),
    { name: "saachi-wishlist" }
  )
);

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES,
  type AdminCourse,
  type AdminCourseStatus,
  type AdminCategory
} from "../dummy-data-admin-courses";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Admin-Courses / Categories API (Doc 3).
let COURSES_STATE: AdminCourse[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES];
let CATEGORIES_STATE: AdminCategory[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES];

export const adminCourseKeys = {
  all: ["admin", "courses"] as const,
  moderationQueue: ["admin", "courses", "moderation-queue"] as const,
  catalog: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "courses", "catalog", filters] as const,
  categories: ["admin", "categories"] as const
};

export function useAdminModerationQueue() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.moderationQueue,
    queryFn: async () => delay(COURSES_STATE.filter((c) => c.status === "pending_review"))
  });
}

export type AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {
  search?: string;
  categoryId?: string;
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminCourseCatalog(filters: AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.catalog(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...COURSES_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter((c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.instructorName.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      }
      if (filters.categoryId) items = items.filter((c) => c.categoryId === filters.categoryId);
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((c) => c.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useModerateCourse() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({
      id,
      decision,
      reason
    }: {
      id: string;
      decision: "published" | "rejected";
      reason?: string;
    }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) =>
        c.id === id ? { ...c, status: decision as AdminCourseStatus, rejectionReason: reason } : c
      );
      return delay({ id, decision }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useToggleCourseFlag() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, field }: { id: string; field: "featured" | "pinned" }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, [field]: !c[field] } : c));
      return delay({ id, field }, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminCategoryList() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...CATEGORIES_STATE])
  });
}

export function useCreateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      const slug = input.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");
      const created: AdminCategory = { id: slug, name: input.name, slug, courseCount: 0, tags: input.tags };
      CATEGORIES_STATE = [...CATEGORIES_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, name, tags }: { id: string; name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, name, tags } : c));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminFinance.ts

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TRANSACTIONS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_PAYOUTS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_REFUNDS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TAX_RULES,
  type AdminTransaction,
  type AdminPayout,
  type PayoutStatus,
  type AdminRefund,
  type RefundStatus,
  type TaxRule
} from "../dummy-data-admin-finance";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Finance/Payouts/Refunds/Tax API (Doc 3).
let TRANSACTIONS_STATE: AdminTransaction[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TRANSACTIONS];
let PAYOUTS_STATE: AdminPayout[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_PAYOUTS];
let REFUNDS_STATE: AdminRefund[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_REFUNDS];
let TAX_RULES_STATE: TaxRule[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TAX_RULES];

export const adminFinanceKeys = {
  all: ["admin", "finance"] as const,
  transactions: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "transactions", filters] as const,
  payouts: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "payouts", filters] as const,
  refunds: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "refunds", filters] as const,
  taxRules: ["admin", "finance", "tax-rules"] as const
};

export type TransactionFilters = {
  search?: string;
  status?: string;
  gateway?: string;
};

export function useAdminTransactions(filters: TransactionFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.transactions(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...TRANSACTIONS_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter(
          (t) =>
            t.courseTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.learnerName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.instructorName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.id.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((t) => t.status === filters.status);
      if (filters.gateway) items = items.filter((t) => t.gateway === filters.gateway);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export type PayoutFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminPayouts(filters: PayoutFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.payouts(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...PAYOUTS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((p) => p.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdatePayoutStatus() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: PayoutStatus }) => {
      PAYOUTS_STATE = PAYOUTS_STATE.map((p) =>
        p.id === id
          ? { ...p, status, processedAt: status === "paid" ? new Date().toISOString() : p.processedAt }
          : p
      );
      return delay({ id, status }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export type RefundFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminRefunds(filters: RefundFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.refunds(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...REFUNDS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((r) => r.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useResolveRefund() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: RefundStatus }) => {
      REFUNDS_STATE = REFUNDS_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, status } : r));
      if (status === "approved") {
        const refund = REFUNDS_STATE.find((r) => r.id === id);
        if (refund) {
          TRANSACTIONS_STATE = TRANSACTIONS_STATE.map((t) =>
            t.id === refund.transactionId ? { ...t, status: "refunded" as const } : t
          );
        }
      }
      return delay({ id, status }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminTaxRules() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...TAX_RULES_STATE])
  });
}

export function useUpdateTaxRule() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, ratePercent, active }: { id: string; ratePercent: number; active: boolean }) => {
      TAX_RULES_STATE = TAX_RULES_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ratePercent, active } : r));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules });
    }
  });
}

export function useCreateTaxRule() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { region: string; ratePercent: number; appliesTo: string }) => {
      const created: TaxRule = {
        id: `tax_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        region: input.region,
        ratePercent: input.ratePercent,
        appliesTo: input.appliesTo,
        active: true
      };
      TAX_RULES_STATE = [...TAX_RULES_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules });
    }
  });
}


================================================================================
FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminMarketing.ts
================================================================================
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_BANNERS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_COUPONS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_PROMOTION_SALES,
  type AdminBanner,
  type AdminCoupon,
  type PromotionSale
} from "../dummy-data-admin-marketing";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Marketing API (Doc 3) once available.
let BANNERS_STATE: AdminBanner[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_BANNERS];
let COUPONS_STATE: AdminCoupon[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_COUPONS];
let PROMOTION_SALES_STATE: PromotionSale[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_PROMOTION_SALES];

export const adminMarketingKeys = {
  banners: ["admin", "marketing", "banners"] as const,
  coupons: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "marketing", "coupons", filters] as const,
  promotionSales: ["admin", "marketing", "promotion-sales"] as const
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Banners
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function useAdminBanners() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...BANNERS_STATE].sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order))
  });
}

export function useCreateBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Omit<AdminBanner, "id" | "order">) => {
      const created: AdminBanner = {
        ...input,
        id: `ban_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        order: BANNERS_STATE.length + 1
      };
      BANNERS_STATE = [...BANNERS_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, ...patch }: Partial<AdminBanner> & { id: string }) => {
      BANNERS_STATE = BANNERS_STATE.map((b) => (b.id === id ? { ...b, ...patch } : b));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      BANNERS_STATE = BANNERS_STATE.filter((b) => b.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

export function useReorderBanner() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, direction }: { id: string; direction: "up" | "down" }) => {
      const sorted = [...BANNERS_STATE].sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order);
      const index = sorted.findIndex((b) => b.id === id);
      const swapWith = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
      if (swapWith < 0 || swapWith >= sorted.length) return delay({ id }, 100);
      const a = sorted[index];
      const b = sorted[swapWith];
      const aOrder = a.order;
      a.order = b.order;
      b.order = aOrder;
      BANNERS_STATE = sorted;
      return delay({ id }, 250);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.banners });
    }
  });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Coupons
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export type CouponFilters = {
  search?: string;
  active?: boolean;
};

export function useAdminCoupons(filters: CouponFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.coupons(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...COUPONS_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter((c) => c.code.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      }
      if (typeof filters.active === "boolean") items = items.filter((c) => c.active === filters.active);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useCreateCoupon() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Omit<AdminCoupon, "id" | "usageCount">) => {
      const created: AdminCoupon = {
        ...input,
        id: `cpn_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        usageCount: 0
      };
      COUPONS_STATE = [...COUPONS_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "marketing", "coupons"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useToggleCouponActive() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      COUPONS_STATE = COUPONS_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, active: !c.active } : c));
      return delay({ id }, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "marketing", "coupons"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteCoupon() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      COUPONS_STATE = COUPONS_STATE.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "marketing", "coupons"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminPromotionSales() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminMarketingKeys.promotionSales,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...PROMOTION_SALES_STATE])
  });
}

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_REVIEWS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_FORUM_POSTS,
  type FlaggedReview,
  type FlaggedForumPost,
  type FlagStatus
} from "../dummy-data-admin-mod-support";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Content Moderation API (Doc 3).
let FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE: FlaggedReview[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_REVIEWS];
let FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE: FlaggedForumPost[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_FLAGGED_FORUM_POSTS];

export const adminModerationKeys = {
  reviews: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "moderation", "reviews", filters] as const,
  forum: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "moderation", "forum", filters] as const
};

export type ModerationFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useFlaggedReviews(filters: ModerationFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminModerationKeys.reviews(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((r) => r.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: FlagStatus }) => {
      FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE = FLAGGED_REVIEWS_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, status } : r));
      return delay({ id, status }, 350);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "moderation", "reviews"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useFlaggedForumPosts(filters: ModerationFilters = {}) {

    queryKey: adminModerationKeys.forum(filters),

      let items = [...FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((p) => p.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useResolveFlaggedForumPost() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: FlagStatus }) => {
      FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE = FLAGGED_FORUM_STATE.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, status } : p));
      return delay({ id, status }, 350);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "moderation", "forum"] });

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TICKETS,
  type SupportTicket,
  type TicketStatus,
  type TicketMessage
} from "../dummy-data-admin-mod-support";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Support/Helpdesk API (Doc 3).
let TICKETS_STATE: SupportTicket[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TICKETS];

export const adminSupportKeys = {
  list: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "support", "tickets", filters] as const,
  detail: (id: string) => ["admin", "support", "tickets", "detail", id] as const
};

export type TicketFilters = {
  search?: string;
  status?: string;
  priority?: string;
};

export function useAdminTickets(filters: TicketFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminSupportKeys.list(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...TICKETS_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter(
          (t) => t.subject.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.requesterName.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((t) => t.status === filters.status);
      if (filters.priority) items = items.filter((t) => t.priority === filters.priority);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminTicketDetail(id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(id),
    queryFn: async () => {
      const found = TICKETS_STATE.find((t) => t.id === id);
      if (!found) throw new Error("Ticket not found");
      return delay(found);
    },
    enabled: Boolean(id)
  });
}

export function useUpdateTicketStatus() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: TicketStatus }) => {
      TICKETS_STATE = TICKETS_STATE.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, status } : t));
      return delay({ id, status }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "support", "tickets"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(variables.id) });
    }
  });
}

export function useAssignTicket() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, assignedTo }: { id: string; assignedTo: string }) => {
      TICKETS_STATE = TICKETS_STATE.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, assignedTo } : t));
      return delay({ id, assignedTo }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "support", "tickets"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(variables.id) });
    }
  });
}

export function useAddTicketMessage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, body, isInternal }: { id: string; body: string; isInternal: boolean }) => {
      const message: TicketMessage = {
        id: `m_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        authorName: "Admin",
        isInternal,
        isStaff: true,
        body,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      TICKETS_STATE = TICKETS_STATE.map((t) =>
        t.id === id ? { ...t, messages: [...t.messages, message], status: t.status === "open" ? "in_progress" : t.status } : t
      );
      return delay(message, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "support", "tickets"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminSupportKeys.detail(variables.id) });
    }
  });
}

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminTicketDetail.ts

"use client";

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

type TicketDetailStatus = "open" | "in_progress" | "resolved" | "closed";
type TicketDetailPriority = "low" | "medium" | "high" | "urgent";
type ThreadAuthorType = "requester" | "staff" | "internal_note";

type TicketThreadEntry = {
  id: string;
  authorType: ThreadAuthorType;
  authorName: string;
  message: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

type TicketDetailRecord = {
  id: string;
  subject: string;
  requesterName: string;
  category: string;
  status: TicketDetailStatus;
  priority: TicketDetailPriority;
  assignedTo?: string;
  slaDueAt: string;
  createdAt: string;
  thread: TicketThreadEntry[];
};

type CannedResponse = { title: string; body: string };

const CANNED_RESPONSES: CannedResponse[] = [
  { title: "Ack - looking into it", body: "Thanks for reaching out - we're looking into this now and will update you shortly." },
  { title: "Resolved - closing", body: "This issue has been resolved on our end. Let us know if you run into it again." },
  { title: "Need more info", body: "Could you share a bit more detail (screenshots, steps to reproduce) so we can help faster?" }
];

const MOCK_TICKETS: Record<string, TicketDetailRecord> = {};

function buildFallbackTicket(id: string): TicketDetailRecord {
  return {
    id,
    subject: "Support request",
    requesterName: "Demo Requester",
    category: "General",
    status: "open",
    priority: "medium",
    slaDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    thread: []
  };
}

let ticketStore: Record<string, TicketDetailRecord> = { ...MOCK_TICKETS };

async function fetchTicketDetail(id: string): Promise<TicketDetailRecord> {
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
  if (!ticketStore[id]) {
    ticketStore[id] = buildFallbackTicket(id);
  }
  return ticketStore[id];
}

export function useAdminTicketDetailPage(id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "ticket-detail", id],
    queryFn: () => fetchTicketDetail(id),
    enabled: Boolean(id)
  });
}

export function useAdminCannedResponses() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "canned-responses"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
      return CANNED_RESPONSES;
    }
  });
}

export function useReplyToTicket() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { id: string; message: string }) => {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
      const current = ticketStore[input.id] ?? buildFallbackTicket(input.id);
      const updated: TicketDetailRecord = {
        ...current,
        status: current.status === "open" ? "in_progress" : current.status,
        thread: [
          ...current.thread,
          { id: `msg_${Date.now()}`, authorType: "staff", authorName: "Admin", message: input.message, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() }
        ]
      };
      ticketStore[input.id] = updated;
      return updated;
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "ticket-detail", data.id] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "tickets"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useAddInternalNote() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { id: string; note: string }) => {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
      const current = ticketStore[input.id] ?? buildFallbackTicket(input.id);
      const updated: TicketDetailRecord = {
        ...current,
        thread: [
          ...current.thread,
          { id: `note_${Date.now()}`, authorType: "internal_note", authorName: "Admin", message: input.note, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() }
        ]
      };
      ticketStore[input.id] = updated;
      return updated;
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "ticket-detail", data.id] });
    }
  });
}

export function useResolveTicket() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { id: string; status: TicketDetailStatus }) => {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 250));
      const current = ticketStore[input.id] ?? buildFallbackTicket(input.id);
      const updated: TicketDetailRecord = { ...current, status: input.status };
      ticketStore[input.id] = updated;
      return updated;
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "ticket-detail", data.id] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "tickets"] });
    }
  });
}

"use client";

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

type UserDetailRole = "learner" | "instructor" | "corporate" | "admin";
type UserDetailStatus = "active" | "suspended" | "banned" | "pending";

type UserActivityLogEntry = {
  id: string;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

type UserDetailRecord = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  avatarUrl?: string;
  role: UserDetailRole;
  status: UserDetailStatus;
  location?: string;
  joinedAt: string;
  lastActiveAt: string;
  activityLog: UserActivityLogEntry[];
};

const MOCK_USER_DETAILS: Record<string, UserDetailRecord> = {
  usr_1001: {
    id: "usr_1001",
    fullName: "Hassan Raza",
    email: "hassan.raza@example.com",
    role: "instructor",
    status: "active",
    location: "Lahore, Pakistan",
    joinedAt: "2026-02-14T00:00:00Z",
    lastActiveAt: "2026-08-07T09:30:00Z",
    activityLog: [
      { id: "act_1", description: "Published course \"AI Automation with n8n\"", createdAt: "2026-07-30T10:00:00Z" },
      { id: "act_2", description: "Requested payout PAYOUT-2026-0913", createdAt: "2026-08-05T10:12:00Z" },
      { id: "act_3", description: "Responded to a learner review", createdAt: "2026-08-01T14:20:00Z" }
    ]
  }
};

function buildFallbackUser(id: string): UserDetailRecord {
  return {
    id,
    fullName: "Demo User",
    email: `${id}@example.com`,
    role: "learner",
    status: "active",
    location: "Unknown",
    joinedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
    lastActiveAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    activityLog: []
  };
}

let userDetailStore: Record<string, UserDetailRecord> = { ...MOCK_USER_DETAILS };

async function fetchUserDetail(id: string): Promise<UserDetailRecord> {
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 350));
  if (!userDetailStore[id]) {
    userDetailStore[id] = buildFallbackUser(id);
  }
  return userDetailStore[id];
}

export function useAdminUserDetailPage(id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "user-detail", id],
    queryFn: () => fetchUserDetail(id),
    enabled: Boolean(id)
  });
}

export function useUpdateUserStatus() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { id: string; status: UserDetailStatus }) => {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
      const current = userDetailStore[input.id] ?? buildFallbackUser(input.id);
      const updated: UserDetailRecord = {
        ...current,
        status: input.status,
        activityLog: [
          {
            id: `act_${Date.now()}`,
            description: `Status changed to "${input.status}"`,
            createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
          },
          ...current.activityLog
        ]
      };
      userDetailStore[input.id] = updated;
      return updated;
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "user-detail", data.id] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "users"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateUserRole() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { id: string; role: UserDetailRole }) => {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
      const current = userDetailStore[input.id] ?? buildFallbackUser(input.id);
      const updated: UserDetailRecord = {
        ...current,
        role: input.role,
        activityLog: [
          {
            id: `act_${Date.now()}`,
            description: `Role changed to "${input.role}"`,
            createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
          },
          ...current.activityLog
        ]
      };
      userDetailStore[input.id] = updated;
      return updated;
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "user-detail", data.id] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin", "users"] });
    }
  });
}

================================================================================
FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAuth.ts
================================================================================
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { authKeys } from "../query-keys";
import type { Session } from "../types";
import type { LoginInput, RegisterInput } from "@saachi/utils";
import { DUMMY_SESSION } from "../dummy-data";

const SESSION_COOKIE = "saachi_session";
const SESSION_STORAGE_KEY = "saachi_demo_session";

function setSessionCookie() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  document.cookie = `${SESSION_COOKIE}=demo_token; path=/; max-age=${60 * 60 * 24 * 7}`;
}

function clearSessionCookie() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  document.cookie = `${SESSION_COOKIE}=; path=/; max-age=0`;
}

function readStoredSession(): Session | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as Session;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function storeSession(session: Session) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  window.localStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(session));
}

function clearStoredSession() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  window.localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
}

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 500): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: Demo mode. There is no backend yet, so login/register accept ANY
// email/password and create a local mock session. Swap these mutationFns
// for real apiRequest(...) calls once the NestJS backend is available.

export function useSession() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: authKeys.session,
    queryFn: async () => readStoredSession(),
    staleTime: 60 * 1000
  });
}

export function useLogin() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: LoginInput) => {
      const session: Session = {
        ...DUMMY_SESSION,
        email: input.email || DUMMY_SESSION.email
      };
      return delay(session);
    },
    onSuccess: (session) => {
      storeSession(session);
      setSessionCookie();
      queryClient.setQueryData(authKeys.session, session);
    }
  });
}

export function useRegister() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: RegisterInput) => {
      const session: Session = {
        ...DUMMY_SESSION,
        fullName: input.fullName,
        email: input.email,
        role: input.role === "instructor" ? "instructor" : "learner"
      };
      return delay(session);
    },
    onSuccess: (session) => {
      storeSession(session);
      setSessionCookie();
      queryClient.setQueryData(authKeys.session, session);
    }
  });
}

export function useLogout() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      clearStoredSession();
      clearSessionCookie();
      return delay(undefined, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.clear();
    }
  });
}

================================================================================
FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useCourses.ts
================================================================================
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { courseKeys } from "../query-keys";
import type { Course, CourseDetail, Paginated } from "../types";
import { DUMMY_COURSES, type DummyCourse } from "../dummy-data";

export type CourseListFilters = {
  category?: string;
  search?: string;
  priceMin?: number;
  priceMax?: number;
  level?: string;
  language?: string;
  sort?: string;
  page?: number;
};

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 400): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

function toSummary(course: DummyCourse): Course {
  return {
    id: course.id,
    slug: course.slug,
    title: course.title,
    subtitle: course.subtitle,
    thumbnailUrl: course.thumbnailUrl,
    price: course.price,
    currency: course.currency,
    rating: course.rating,
    ratingCount: course.ratingCount,
    instructor: course.instructor,
    status: course.status
  };
}

export function useCourseList(filters: CourseListFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: courseKeys.list(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...DUMMY_COURSES];

      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter(
          (c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.instructor.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      if (filters.category) {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.categoryId === filters.category);
      }
      if (filters.level) {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.level === filters.level);
      }
      if (filters.language) {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.language === filters.language);
      }
      if (typeof filters.priceMin === "number") {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.price >= (filters.priceMin as number));
      }
      if (typeof filters.priceMax === "number") {
        items = items.filter((c) => c.price <= (filters.priceMax as number));
      }

      switch (filters.sort) {
        case "newest":
          items = [...items].reverse();
          break;
        case "price_asc":
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price);
          break;
        case "price_desc":
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => b.price - a.price);
          break;
        case "rating":
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => b.rating - a.rating);
          break;
        case "popular":
        default:
          items = [...items].sort((a, b) => b.ratingCount - a.ratingCount);
      }

      const page = filters.page ?? 1;
      const pageSize = 12;
      const start = (page - 1) * pageSize;
      const pageItems = items.slice(start, start + pageSize).map(toSummary);

      return delay<Paginated<Course>>({
        items: pageItems,
        total: items.length,
        page,
        pageSize
      });
    }
  });
}

export function useCourseDetail(idOrSlug: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: courseKeys.detail(idOrSlug),
    queryFn: async () => {
      const found = DUMMY_COURSES.find((c) => c.slug === idOrSlug || c.id === idOrSlug);
      if (!found) throw new Error("Course not found");
      const { categoryId, level, language, ...detail } = found;
      return delay<CourseDetail>(detail);
    },
    enabled: Boolean(idOrSlug)
  });
}

export function useCreateCourse() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Course>) => {
      const created: Course = {
        id: `crs_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        slug: (input.slug as string) ?? "new-course",
        title: input.title ?? "Untitled course",
        price: input.price ?? 0,
        currency: input.currency ?? "USD",
        rating: 0,
        ratingCount: 0,
        instructor: input.instructor ?? { id: "ins_demo", fullName: "Demo Instructor" },
        status: "draft"
      };
      return delay(created);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: courseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateCourse(id: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Course>) => {
      const existing = DUMMY_COURSES.find((c) => c.id === id);
      const base: Course = existing
        ? toSummary(existing)
        : {
            id,
            slug: id,
            title: "Untitled",
            price: 0,
            currency: "USD",
            rating: 0,
            ratingCount: 0,
            instructor: { id: "ins_demo", fullName: "Demo Instructor" },
            status: "draft"
          };
      const updated: Course = { ...base, ...input };
      return delay(updated);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: courseKeys.detail(id) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: courseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export type TransactionStatus = "completed" | "pending" | "failed" | "refunded";

export type AdminTransaction = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  learnerName: string;
  instructorName: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
  commission: number;
  netToInstructor: number;
  gateway: "stripe" | "paypal" | "easypaisa" | "jazzcash" | "bank_transfer";
  status: TransactionStatus;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type PayoutStatus = "pending" | "approved" | "paid" | "rejected";

  id: string;
  instructorName: string;
  instructorId: string;

  requestedAt: string;
  processedAt?: string;
};

  requestedAt: string;
};

};

  endsAt: string;

};

  id: string;

}

export type FlagStatus = "pending" | "removed" | "dismissed" | "warned";

export type FlaggedReview = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  authorName: string;
  comment: string;
  rating: number;
  flagReason: string;
  flaggedBy: string;
  status: FlagStatus;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type FlaggedForumPost = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  authorName: string;
  body: string;
  flagReason: string;
  flaggedBy: string;
  status: FlagStatus;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type TicketStatus = "open" | "in_progress" | "resolved" | "closed";
export type TicketPriority = "low" | "medium" | "high" | "urgent";

export type TicketMessage = {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  isInternal: boolean;
  isStaff: boolean;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type SupportTicket = {
  id: string;
  subject: string;
  requesterName: string;
  requesterEmail: string;
  category: string;
  priority: TicketPriority;
  status: TicketStatus;
  assignedTo?: string;
  createdAt: string;
  slaDueAt: string;
  messages: TicketMessage[];
};

function hoursAgoIso(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() - n * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

function hoursFromNowIso(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() + n * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

export type AdminUserRole = "learner" | "instructor" | "corporate" | "admin";
export type AdminUserStatus = "active" | "suspended" | "banned" | "pending";

export type AdminUser = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  avatarUrl?: string;
  role: AdminUserRole;
  status: AdminUserStatus;
  joinedAt: string;
  lastActiveAt: string;
  coursesEnrolled?: number;
  coursesPublished?: number;
  totalSpend?: number;
  totalEarnings?: number;
  location?: string;
};

export type AdminActivityLogEntry = {
  id: string;
  action: string;
  detail: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type AdminAuditLogEntry = {
  id: string;
  actor: string;
  action: string;
  target: string;
  createdAt: string;
  ip: string;
};

function img(seed: string): string {
  return `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(seed)}/150/150`;
}

function daysAgo(n: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.now() - n * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
}

    { id: `${userId}_a2`, action: "Profile updated", detail: "Changed display name and avatar", createdAt: daysAgo(4) },
    { id: `${userId}_a3`, action: "Course enrolled", detail: "Enrolled in \"Prompt Engineering Fundamentals\"", createdAt: daysAgo(10) },
    { id: `${userId}_a4`, action: "Payment completed", detail: "Order ORD-8H2K1 - $29.00 via Stripe", createdAt: daysAgo(10) },
    { id: `${userId}_a5`, action: "Account created", detail: "Registered via email/password", createdAt: daysAgo(95) }
  ];
}

export function getAdminAuditLog(userId: string): AdminAuditLogEntry[] {
  return [
    { id: `${userId}_l1`, actor: "system", action: "flagged for review", target: userId, createdAt: daysAgo(21), ip: "10.4.2.19" },
    { id: `${userId}_l2`, actor: "admin@saachi.ai", action: "sent warning email", target: userId, createdAt: daysAgo(20), ip: "internal" }
  ];
}

import type { Course, CourseDetail, Session } from "./types";

function img(seed: string, w: number, h: number): string {
  return `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(seed)}/${w}/${h}`;
}

export const DUMMY_SESSION: Session = {
  id: "usr_demo_001",
  fullName: "Ayesha Khan",
  email: "demo@saachi.ai",
  role: "learner",
  avatarUrl: img("ayesha-khan-demo", 150, 150)
};

export type DummyCourse = CourseDetail & {
  categoryId: string;
  level: "beginner" | "intermediate" | "advanced";
  language: "en" | "ur";
};

function toCourseSummary(course: DummyCourse): Course {
  return {
    id: course.id,
    slug: course.slug,
    title: course.title,
    subtitle: course.subtitle,
    thumbnailUrl: course.thumbnailUrl,
    price: course.price,
    currency: course.currency,
    rating: course.rating,
    ratingCount: course.ratingCount,
    instructor: course.instructor,
    status: course.status
  };
}

export const DUMMY_COURSES: DummyCourse[] = BASE_COURSES.map((course, index) => {
  const others = BASE_COURSES.filter((c) => c.id !== course.id);
  const related = [others[index % others.length], others[(index + 1) % others.length]].filter(Boolean);
  return { ...course, relatedCourses: related.map(toCourseSummary) };
});


================================================================================
FILE: packages\api-client\src\http-client.ts
================================================================================
export type ApiError = {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
};

export class ApiRequestError extends Error {
  code: string;
  fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
  status: number;

  constructor(status: number, error: ApiError) {
    super(error.message);
    this.name = "ApiRequestError";
    this.status = status;
    this.code = error.code;
    this.fieldErrors = error.fieldErrors;
  }
}

type RequestOptions = {
  method?: "GET" | "POST" | "PATCH" | "PUT" | "DELETE";
  body?: unknown;
  params?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
};

function buildUrl(path: string, params?: RequestOptions["params"]): string {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "";
  const url = new URL(path.replace(/^\//, ""), base.endsWith("/") ? base : `${base}/`);
  if (params) {
    Object.entries(params).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
    });
  }
  return url.toString();
}

export async function apiRequest<TResponse>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestOptions = {}
): Promise<TResponse> {
  const { method = "GET", body, params, signal } = options;

  const response = await fetch(buildUrl(path, params), {
    method,
    credentials: "include",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json"
    },
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
    signal
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let errorPayload: ApiError = { code: "unknown_error", message: "Something went wrong" };
    try {
      errorPayload = await response.json();
    } catch {
      // response had no JSON body
    }
    throw new ApiRequestError(response.status, errorPayload);
  }

  if (response.status === 204) return undefined as TResponse;
  return response.json() as Promise<TResponse>;
}
